package com.rtsr.signal

import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry
import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4

import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith

import org.junit.Assert.*

/**
 * Instrumented test, which will execute on an Android device.
 *
 * See [testing documentation](http://d.android.com/tools/testing).
 */
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)
class ExampleInstrumentedTest {
    @Test
    fun useAppContext() {
        // Context of the app under test.
        val appContext = InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().targetContext
        assertEquals("com.example.signal", appContext.packageName)
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.Andriod/app/src/main/java/com/rtsr/signal/models/MainUiState.kt">
package com.rtsr.signal.models

import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.EquipmentDto
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.GraphPointDto
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.LatestReadingResponse
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.ReadingLogDto

data class MainUiState(
    val isAuthorized: Boolean = false,
    val isLoading: Boolean = true,
    val userFullName: String = "",
    val currentScreen: AppScreen = AppScreen.MONITORING,
    val isAutoLoginChecking: Boolean = true,
    val isRefreshing: Boolean = false,
    val devices: List<EquipmentDto> = emptyList(),
    val selectedDeviceId: String? = null,
    val selectedPeriod: PeriodType = PeriodType.ALL_TIME,
    val viewMode: ViewMode = ViewMode.LATEST,
    val lastUpdateTime: String = "",
    val apiAddress: String = "192.168.59.18",
    val apiPort: String = "22",
    val isAddressSaved: Boolean = true,
    val addressErrorResId: Int? = null,
    val portErrorResId: Int? = null,
    val loginErrorMessage: Int? = null,
    val isLoginLoading: Boolean = false,
    val logsList: List<ReadingLogDto> = emptyList(),
    val isConnectionChecking: Boolean = false,
    val connectionStatus: ConnectionStatus = ConnectionStatus.NOT_TESTED,
    val availableParams: List<String> = emptyList(),
    val selectedParamIdentifier: String? = null,
    val currentPage: Int = 1,
    val isPageLoading: Boolean = false,
    val totalCount: Int = 0,
    val graphPoints: List<GraphPointDto> = emptyList(),
    val isGraphLoading: Boolean = false,
    val latestReadings: List<LatestReadingResponse> = emptyList(),
    val isConnected: Boolean = true,
    val useHttps: String = ""
)
</file>

<file path="Signal.Andriod/app/src/main/java/com/rtsr/signal/models/PeriodType.kt">
package com.rtsr.signal.models

import androidx.annotation.StringRes
import com.rtsr.signal.R

enum class PeriodType(@StringRes val resId: Int) {
    ALL_TIME(R.string.period_all_time),
    LAST_MONTH(R.string.period_last_month),
    CURRENT_MONTH(R.string.period_current_month),
    LAST_WEEK(R.string.period_last_week),
    CURRENT_WEEK(R.string.period_current_week),
    YESTERDAY(R.string.period_yesterday),
    TODAY(R.string.period_today),
    HOUR_1(R.string.period_1_hour)
}
</file>

package com.rtsr.signal.models

import android.app.Application
import com.rtsr.signal.services.TokenManager

class SignalApplication : Application() {

    companion object {
        lateinit var tokenManager: TokenManager
            private set
    }

    override fun onCreate() {
        super.onCreate()
        tokenManager = TokenManager(this)
    }
}

<file path="Signal.Andriod/app/src/main/java/com/rtsr/signal/screens/AppNavigationScreen.kt">
package com.rtsr.signal.screens

import android.os.Build
import androidx.annotation.RequiresApi
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Box
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.material3.CircularProgressIndicator
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.collectAsState
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.rememberCoroutineScope
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import com.rtsr.signal.models.AppScreen
import com.rtsr.signal.viewmodel.AppViewModel
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

@RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
@Composable
fun AppNavigationScreen(vm: AppViewModel) {
    val uiState by vm.uiState.collectAsState()

    when {
        uiState.isAutoLoginChecking -> {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
            ) {
                CircularProgressIndicator()
            }
        }

        !uiState.isAuthorized -> {
            when (uiState.currentScreen){
                AppScreen.SETTINGS -> {
                    SettingsScreen(
                        uiState = vm.uiState.collectAsState().value,
                        onSaveClick = { address, port, protocol -> vm.saveSettings(address, port, protocol) },
                        onFieldsValueChange = { address, port, protocol ->
                            vm.onSettingsInputChanged(
                                address,
                                port,
                                protocol
                            )
                        },
                        onCheckConnectionClick = { address, port, protocol ->
                            vm.checkConnection(
                                address,
                                port,
                                protocol
                            )
                        },
                        onBack = { screen -> vm.navigateTo(screen) },
                        screen = AppScreen.MONITORING
                    )
                }
                else -> {
                    AuthorizationScreen(
                        uiState = uiState,
                        onLoginClick = { login, password ->
                            vm.login(login, password)
                        },
                        toSettingsClick = { targetScreen: AppScreen ->
                            vm.navigateTo(targetScreen)
                        },
                        screen = AppScreen.SETTINGS
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        else -> {
            MainNavigationContainer(vm)
        }
    }
}
</file>

package com.rtsr.signal.screens

import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.*
import androidx.compose.foundation.text.KeyboardOptions
import androidx.compose.material.icons.Icons
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Favorite
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.FavoriteBorder
import androidx.compose.material.icons.filled.Visibility
import androidx.compose.material.icons.outlined.VisibilityOff
import androidx.compose.material3.*
import androidx.compose.runtime.*
import androidx.compose.ui.Alignment
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.res.stringResource
import androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontWeight
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.KeyboardType
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.PasswordVisualTransformation
import androidx.compose.ui.text.input.VisualTransformation
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.sp
import com.rtsr.signal.R
import com.rtsr.signal.models.AppScreen
import com.rtsr.signal.models.MainUiState
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch


@Composable
fun AuthorizationScreen(
    uiState: MainUiState,
    onLoginClick: suspend (String, String) -> Unit,
    toSettingsClick: (AppScreen) -> Unit,
    screen: AppScreen
) {
    var login by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var password by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var passwordVisible by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    val isLoaderActive = uiState.isLoginLoading

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(24.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.login_title),
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.displayMedium,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
            letterSpacing = 2.sp
        )

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(48.dp))

        OutlinedTextField(
            value = login,
            onValueChange = { login = it },
            label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.login_hint)) },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            enabled = !isLoaderActive,
            isError = uiState.loginErrorMessage != null
        )

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

        OutlinedTextField(
            value = password,
            onValueChange = { password = it },
            label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.password_hint)) },
            trailingIcon = {
                val image = if (passwordVisible) Icons.Default.Visibility else Icons.Outlined.VisibilityOff
                IconButton(
                    onClick = { passwordVisible = !passwordVisible },
                    enabled = !isLoaderActive
                ) {
                    Icon(image, contentDescription = if (passwordVisible) "Скрыть" else "Показать")
                }
            },
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            singleLine = true,
            enabled = !isLoaderActive,
            visualTransformation = if (passwordVisible) VisualTransformation.None else PasswordVisualTransformation(),
            keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Password),
            isError = uiState.loginErrorMessage != null
        )

        uiState.loginErrorMessage?.let { resId ->
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
            Text(
                text = stringResource(resId),
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium
            )
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(32.dp))

        Button(
            onClick = {
                scope.launch {
                    onLoginClick(login.trim(), password.trim())
                }
            },
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .height(50.dp),
            enabled = !isLoaderActive
        ) {
            if (isLoaderActive) {
                CircularProgressIndicator(
                    modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp),
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimary,
                    strokeWidth = 2.5.dp
                )
            } else {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.btn_login),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

        TextButton(
            onClick = { toSettingsClick(screen) },
            enabled = !isLoaderActive
        ) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.menu_settings),
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun ContactScreen(){
    val gitUri = "https://github.com/Skverno-Slov"
    val email = "zaharlukanin19@gmail.com"

    val deviceModel = "${Build.MANUFACTURER} ${Build.MODEL}"
    val androidVersion = Build.VERSION.RELEASE

    val subject = stringResource(R.string.mail_subject)
    val emailBodyTemplate = stringResource(R.string.mail_body, deviceModel, androidVersion).trimIndent()

    val encodedSubject = Uri.encode(subject)
    val encodedBody = Uri.encode(emailBodyTemplate)
    val mailtoUrl = "mailto:$email?subject=$encodedSubject&body=$encodedBody"

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(24.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.str_title_contact),
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 32.dp, top = 16.dp)
        )

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp))
        {
            Text(
                text = "github",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )

            LinkedText(gitUri, stringResource(R.string.str_open_web))

        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp))
        {
            Text(
                text = "E-mail",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
            )
            LinkedText(mailtoUrl, stringResource(R.string.str_write_mail))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LinkedText(dynamicUrl: String, linkLabel: String) {
    val annotatedString = buildAnnotatedString {
        withLink(
            LinkAnnotation.Url(
                url = dynamicUrl,
                styles = TextLinkStyles(
                    style = SpanStyle(
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary,
                        textDecoration = TextDecoration.Underline
                    )
                )
            )
        ) {
            append(linkLabel)
        }

    }

    BasicText(text = annotatedString, style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium)
}

@RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class, ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)
@Composable
fun MainNavigationContainer(vm: AppViewModel) {
    val uiState by vm.uiState.collectAsState()
    val drawerState = rememberDrawerState(initialValue = DrawerValue.Closed)
    val scope = rememberCoroutineScope()

    val pagerState = rememberPagerState(pageCount = { 2 })

    ModalNavigationDrawer(
        drawerState = drawerState,
        drawerContent = {
            ModalDrawerSheet {
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
                NavigationDrawerItem(
                    icon = { Icon(Icons.Default.Home, contentDescription = null) },
                    label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.menu_monitoring)) },
                    selected = uiState.currentScreen == AppScreen.MONITORING,
                    onClick = {
                        vm.navigateTo(AppScreen.MONITORING)
                        scope.launch { drawerState.close() }
                    }
                )
                NavigationDrawerItem(
                    icon = { Icon(Icons.Default.Settings, contentDescription = null) },
                    label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.menu_settings)) },
                    selected = uiState.currentScreen == AppScreen.SETTINGS,
                    onClick = {
                        vm.navigateTo(AppScreen.SETTINGS)
                        scope.launch { drawerState.close() }
                    }
                )
                NavigationDrawerItem(
                    icon = { Icon(Icons.Default.Info, contentDescription = null) },
                    label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.menu_report_issue)) },
                    selected = false,
                    onClick = {
                        vm.navigateTo(AppScreen.CONTACT)
                        scope.launch { drawerState.close() }
                    }
                )
                NavigationDrawerItem(
                    icon = { Icon(Icons.Default.ExitToApp, contentDescription = null) },
                    label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.menu_logout)) },
                    selected = false,
                    onClick = { vm.logout() }
                )
            }
        }
    ) {
        Scaffold(
            topBar = {
                TopAppBar(
                    title = {},
                    navigationIcon = {
                        IconButton(onClick = { scope.launch { drawerState.open() } }) {
                            Icon(Icons.Default.Menu, contentDescription = "", tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimary)
                        }
                    },
                    actions = {
                        Text(
                            text = uiState.userFullName,
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(end = 16.dp),
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimary
                        )
                    },
                    colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(
                        containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant,
                        navigationIconContentColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                        actionIconContentColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant
                    )
                )
            },
            bottomBar = {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant)
                        .windowInsetsPadding(WindowInsets.navigationBars)
                        .padding(8.dp),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.CenterStart
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.footer_copyright),
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimary
                    )
                }
            }
        ) { paddingValues ->
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(paddingValues)
            ) {
                when (uiState.currentScreen) {
                    AppScreen.SETTINGS -> {
                        SettingsScreen(
                            uiState = vm.uiState.collectAsState().value,
                            onSaveClick = { address, port, protocol -> vm.saveSettings(address, port, protocol) },
                            onFieldsValueChange = { address, port, protocol -> vm.onSettingsInputChanged(address, port, protocol) },
                            onCheckConnectionClick = { address, port, protocol -> vm.checkConnection(address, port, protocol) },
                            onBack = { screen -> vm.navigateTo(screen) },
                            screen = AppScreen.MONITORING
                        )
                    }
                    AppScreen.CONTACT ->{
                        ContactScreen()
                    }
                    AppScreen.MONITORING -> {
                        when {
                            uiState.isLoading -> {
                                LoadingStateScreen()
                            }

                            !uiState.isConnected -> {
                                EmptyStateScreen({ vm.loadAllData() })
                            }

                            else -> {
                                PullToRefreshBox(
                                    isRefreshing = uiState.isRefreshing,
                                    onRefresh = { vm.refreshData() },
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()
                                ) {
                                    HorizontalPager(
                                        state = pagerState,
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .fillMaxSize()
                                    ) { page ->
                                        when (page) {
                                            0 -> DashboardScreen(
                                                vm.uiState.collectAsState().value,
                                                { period -> vm.selectPeriod(period) },
                                                { isGraph -> vm.toggleViewMode(isGraph) },
                                                { paramId -> vm.selectParamIdentifier(paramId) },
                                                { pageNum -> vm.changePage(pageNum) },
                                                {paramId -> vm.getParamColor(paramId)}
                                            )

                                            1 -> DeviceListScreen(
                                                uiState,
                                                { deviceId ->
                                                    vm.selectDevice(deviceId)
                                                    scope.launch { pagerState.animateScrollToPage(0) }
                                                }
                                            )
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}


@RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun DashboardScreen(
    uiState: MainUiState,
    onPeriodSelected: (PeriodType) -> Unit,
    onViewModeChanged: (ViewMode) -> Unit,
    onParamSelected: (String?) -> Unit,
    onPageSelected: (Int) -> Unit,
    getParamColor: (String) -> Color
) {
    val currentDevice = uiState.devices.find { it.serialNumber == uiState.selectedDeviceId }
    val colorScheme = MaterialTheme.colorScheme

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
    ) {
        item {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
            Text(text = stringResource(R.string.data_actual_at, uiState.lastUpdateTime), style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall, color = Color.Gray)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
            Text(text = currentDevice?.name ?: "—", style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium, fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

            if(uiState.viewMode != ViewMode.LATEST) {
                val isGraphView = uiState.viewMode == ViewMode.GRAPH
                LazyRow(
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth()
                ) {
                    if (!isGraphView) {
                        item {
                            FilterChip(
                                selected = uiState.selectedParamIdentifier == null,
                                onClick = { onParamSelected(null) },
                                label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.str_all)) },
                                border = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipBorder(
                                    enabled = true,
                                    selected = uiState.selectedParamIdentifier == null,
                                    borderColor = Color.Gray,
                                    selectedBorderColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }
                    items(uiState.availableParams) { paramId ->
                        val paramColor = remember(paramId) { getParamColor(paramId) }
                        FilterChip(
                            selected = uiState.selectedParamIdentifier == paramId,
                            onClick = { onParamSelected(paramId) },
                            label = { Text(paramId) },
                            border = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipBorder(
                                enabled = true,
                                selected = uiState.selectedParamIdentifier == paramId,
                                borderColor = paramColor,
                                selectedBorderColor = paramColor,
                                selectedBorderWidth = 2.dp
                            )
                        )
                    }
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
            }
        }

        when (uiState.viewMode) {
            ViewMode.GRAPH -> {
                item {
                    if (uiState.isGraphLoading) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(280.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            CircularProgressIndicator()
                        }
                    } else {
                        val filteredPoints =
                            remember(uiState.graphPoints, uiState.selectedParamIdentifier) {
                                if (uiState.selectedParamIdentifier == null) {
                                    uiState.graphPoints
                                } else {
                                    uiState.graphPoints.filter { it.param == uiState.selectedParamIdentifier }
                                }
                            }

                        val pointsByParam = remember(filteredPoints) {
                            filteredPoints.groupBy { it.param }
                        }

                        val modelProducer = remember { CartesianChartModelProducer() }

                        val xDateLabels = remember(filteredPoints) {
                            filteredPoints.map { it.dt }.distinct().sorted()
                        }

                        val hasAnyNumericData = remember(pointsByParam) {
                            pointsByParam.values.any { points ->
                                points.any { it.value?.toFloatOrNull() != null }
                            }
                        }

                        if (pointsByParam.isNotEmpty() && hasAnyNumericData) {
                            LaunchedEffect(pointsByParam, xDateLabels) {
                                if (pointsByParam.isNotEmpty() && xDateLabels.isNotEmpty()) {
                                    modelProducer.runTransaction {
                                        lineSeries {
                                            pointsByParam.forEach { (_, points) ->
                                                val segments =
                                                    mutableListOf<MutableList<Pair<Float, Float>>>()
                                                var currentSegment =
                                                    mutableListOf<Pair<Float, Float>>()

                                                points.forEach { point ->
                                                    val xValue =
                                                        xDateLabels.indexOf(point.dt).toFloat()
                                                    val yValue = point.value?.toFloatOrNull()

                                                    if (yValue != null && xValue >= 0) {
                                                        currentSegment.add(xValue to yValue)
                                                    } else {
                                                        if (currentSegment.isNotEmpty()) {
                                                            segments.add(currentSegment)
                                                            currentSegment = mutableListOf()
                                                        }
                                                    }
                                                }
                                                if (currentSegment.isNotEmpty()) {
                                                    segments.add(currentSegment)
                                                }

                                                segments.forEach { segment ->
                                                    series(
                                                        x = segment.map { it.first },
                                                        y = segment.map { it.second }
                                                    )
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        if (pointsByParam.isNotEmpty() && hasAnyNumericData) {

                            val lineProvider = LineCartesianLayer.LineProvider.series(
                                pointsByParam.keys.map { paramId ->
                                    val paramColor = getParamColor(paramId)
                                    LineCartesianLayer.rememberLine(
                                        fill = LineCartesianLayer.LineFill.single(fill(paramColor))
                                    )
                                }
                            )

                            val density = androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalDensity.current
                            val valueTextSizePx =
                                remember(density) { with(density) { 18.sp.toPx().toInt() } }
                            val dateTextSizePx =
                                remember(density) { with(density) { 12.sp.toPx().toInt() } }

                            val defaultMarker = rememberDefaultCartesianMarker(
                                label = rememberTextComponent(
                                    color = Color.White,
                                    background = rememberShapeComponent(
                                        shape = com.patrykandpatrick.vico.core.common.shape.Shape.Rectangle,
                                        fill = fill(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface.copy(alpha = 0.95f))
                                    ),
                                    lineCount = 3,
                                    truncateAt = TextUtils.TruncateAt.MARQUEE,
                                    textAlignment = Layout.Alignment.ALIGN_CENTER,
                                    padding = Insets(horizontalDp = 12f, verticalDp = 8f),
                                    minWidth = com.patrykandpatrick.vico.core.common.component.TextComponent.MinWidth.fixed(
                                        160f
                                    )
                                ),
                                valueFormatter = { _, targets ->
                                    val lineTarget =
                                        targets.firstOrNull() as? LineCartesianLayerMarkerTarget
                                    val xIndex = lineTarget?.x?.toInt() ?: 0

                                    val rawY = lineTarget?.points?.firstOrNull()?.entry?.y
                                    val yValue = if (rawY != null) {
                                        String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.2f", rawY)
                                            .dropLastWhile { it == '0' }
                                            .dropLastWhile { it == '.' }
                                    } else {
                                        "0.0"
                                    }

                                    val fullDateTime = xDateLabels.getOrNull(xIndex) ?: "—"
                                    val formattedTime = try {
                                        val instant = java.time.Instant.parse(fullDateTime)

                                        val localDateTime = java.time.LocalDateTime.ofInstant(
                                            instant,
                                            java.time.ZoneId.systemDefault()
                                        )

                                        val outputFormatter =
                                            java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy:MM:dd HH:mm:ss")
                                        localDateTime.format(outputFormatter)
                                    } catch (_: Exception) {
                                        try {
                                            val cleanFormat =
                                                fullDateTime.take(19).replace("T", " ")
                                            val inputFormatter =
                                                java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss")
                                            val outputFormatter =
                                                java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy:MM:dd HH:mm:ss")
                                            java.time.LocalDateTime.parse(
                                                cleanFormat,
                                                inputFormatter
                                            ).format(outputFormatter)
                                        } catch (_: Exception) {
                                            fullDateTime
                                        }
                                    }

                                    SpannableStringBuilder().apply {
                                        val valueStart = length
                                        append(yValue)
                                        val valueEnd = length

                                        setSpan(StyleSpan(Typeface.BOLD), valueStart, valueEnd, 0)
                                        setSpan(
                                            ForegroundColorSpan(android.graphics.Color.WHITE),
                                            valueStart,
                                            valueEnd,
                                            0
                                        )
                                        setSpan(
                                            AbsoluteSizeSpan(valueTextSizePx, false),
                                            valueStart,
                                            valueEnd,
                                            0
                                        )

                                        append("\n")

                                        val dateStart = length
                                        append(formattedTime)
                                        val dateEnd = length

                                        setSpan(
                                            ForegroundColorSpan(
                                                android.graphics.Color.parseColor(
                                                    "#A0A0A0"
                                                )
                                            ), dateStart, dateEnd, 0
                                        )
                                        setSpan(
                                            AbsoluteSizeSpan(dateTextSizePx, false),
                                            dateStart,
                                            dateEnd,
                                            0
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            )

                            val chart = rememberCartesianChart(
                                rememberLineCartesianLayer(lineProvider = lineProvider),
                                startAxis = VerticalAxis.rememberStart(
                                    label = rememberTextComponent(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground)
                                ),
                                bottomAxis = HorizontalAxis.rememberBottom(
                                    label = rememberTextComponent(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onBackground),
                                    labelRotationDegrees = 0f,
                                    valueFormatter = { _, value, _ ->
                                        val index = kotlin.math.round(value).toInt()
                                        val fullDateTime = xDateLabels.getOrNull(index) ?: ""

                                        if (fullDateTime.isEmpty()) {
                                            ""
                                        } else {
                                            try {
                                                val instant = java.time.Instant.parse(fullDateTime)
                                                val localDateTime =
                                                    java.time.LocalDateTime.ofInstant(
                                                        instant,
                                                        java.time.ZoneId.systemDefault()
                                                    )
                                                localDateTime.format(
                                                    java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(
                                                        "yyyy.MM.dd"
                                                    )
                                                )
                                            } catch (_: Exception) {
                                                try {
                                                    val cleanFormat =
                                                        fullDateTime.take(19).replace("T", " ")
                                                    val inputFormatter =
                                                        java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(
                                                            "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"
                                                        )
                                                    val outputFormatter =
                                                        java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(
                                                            "yyyy.MM.dd"
                                                        )
                                                    java.time.LocalDateTime.parse(
                                                        cleanFormat,
                                                        inputFormatter
                                                    ).format(outputFormatter)
                                                } catch (_: Exception) {
                                                    fullDateTime.take(10).replace("-", ".")
                                                }
                                            }
                                        }
                                    },
                                    itemPlacer = HorizontalAxis.ItemPlacer.aligned(
                                        spacing = { 4 },
                                        addExtremeLabelPadding = true
                                    )
                                ),
                                marker = defaultMarker
                            )

                            CartesianChartHost(
                                chart = chart,
                                modelProducer = modelProducer,
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .height(320.dp),
                                zoomState = rememberVicoZoomState(
                                    minZoom = Zoom.Content,
                                    maxZoom = Zoom.fixed(8f)
                                )
                            )
                        } else if(pointsByParam.isNotEmpty()) {
                            Column(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .height(320.dp),
                                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Clear,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(48.dp)
                                        .padding(bottom = 8.dp)
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(R.string.err_graph_unavalable),
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                                )
                            }
                        } else{
                            Column(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .height(320.dp),
                                horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Clear,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error,
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .size(48.dp)
                                        .padding(bottom = 8.dp)
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(R.string.err_graf_no_data),
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                                    textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            ViewMode.TABLE -> {
                item {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(4.dp)
                    ) {
                        if (uiState.isPageLoading) {
                            LinearProgressIndicator(
                                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                color = colorScheme.primary,
                                trackColor = Color.Transparent
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }

                item {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(vertical = 8.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.header_param_name),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1.5f)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.header_param_value),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1.5f),
                            textAlign = TextAlign.End
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.header_status_name),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            textAlign = TextAlign.End
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.header_param_date),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(2f),
                            textAlign = TextAlign.End
                        )
                    }
                    HorizontalDivider(color = colorScheme.outlineVariant)
                }

                items(uiState.logsList) { log ->
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .graphicsLayer {
                                alpha = if (uiState.isPageLoading) 0.5f else 1f
                            }
                            .padding(vertical = 10.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = log.readingName,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1.5f)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "${log.readingValue} ${log.readingUnit ?: ""}".trim(),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1.5f),
                            textAlign = TextAlign.End
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = log.readingStatus.trim(),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                            textAlign = TextAlign.End
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = log.writeDateTime.take(16).replace("T", " "),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                            color = Color.Gray,
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(2f),
                            textAlign = TextAlign.End
                        )
                    }
                    HorizontalDivider(color = colorScheme.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.3f))
                }

                if (uiState.logsList.isNotEmpty()) {
                    item {
                        PaginationControl(
                            currentPage = uiState.currentPage,
                            totalCount = uiState.totalCount,
                            pageSize = 8,
                            onPageChanged = { newPage ->
                                if (!uiState.isPageLoading) onPageSelected(newPage)
                            }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            ViewMode.LATEST -> {
                if (uiState.latestReadings.isEmpty() && !uiState.isLoading) {
                    item {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(200.dp),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(text = stringResource(R.string.err_no_actual_data), color = Color.Gray)
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    items(uiState.latestReadings) { reading ->
                        LatestReadingCard(
                            reading = reading,
                            getParamColor = getParamColor
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (uiState.isLoading && uiState.logsList.isEmpty()) {
            item {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(32.dp), contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    CircularProgressIndicator(modifier = Modifier.size(32.dp))
                }
            }
        }

        item {
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
            Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically, horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween) {
                if (uiState.viewMode != ViewMode.LATEST) {
                    var expanded by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
                    Box {
                        Button(onClick = { expanded = true }) {
                            Text(stringResource(uiState.selectedPeriod.resId))
                            Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowDropDown, contentDescription = null)
                        }
                        DropdownMenu(expanded = expanded, onDismissRequest = { expanded = false }) {
                            PeriodType.entries.forEach { period ->
                                DropdownMenuItem(
                                    text = { Text(stringResource(period.resId)) },
                                    onClick = { onPeriodSelected(period); expanded = false })
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                Row(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(), horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Absolute.Right) {
                    IconButton(
                        onClick = { onViewModeChanged(ViewMode.LATEST) },
                        colors = IconButtonDefaults.iconButtonColors(
                            containerColor = if (uiState.viewMode == ViewMode.LATEST) colorScheme.primary else Color.Transparent,
                            contentColor = if (uiState.viewMode == ViewMode.LATEST) colorScheme.onPrimary else colorScheme.onSurface
                        )
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Outlined.Info, contentDescription = "Latest readings")
                    }

                    IconButton(
                        onClick = { onViewModeChanged(ViewMode.GRAPH) },
                        colors = IconButtonDefaults.iconButtonColors(
                            containerColor = if (uiState.viewMode == ViewMode.GRAPH) colorScheme.primary else Color.Transparent,
                            contentColor = if (uiState.viewMode == ViewMode.GRAPH) colorScheme.onPrimary else colorScheme.onSurface
                        )
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Outlined.ShowChart, contentDescription = "Metric/Graph")
                    }

                    IconButton(
                        onClick = { onViewModeChanged(ViewMode.TABLE) },
                        colors = IconButtonDefaults.iconButtonColors(
                            containerColor = if (uiState.viewMode == ViewMode.TABLE) colorScheme.primary else Color.Transparent,
                            contentColor = if (uiState.viewMode == ViewMode.TABLE) colorScheme.onPrimary else colorScheme.onSurface
                        )
                    ) {
                        Icon(Icons.Default.List, contentDescription = "Table/Logs")
                    }
                }
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(24.dp))
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LatestReadingCard(
    reading: LatestReadingResponse,
    getParamColor: (String) -> Color,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    val borderColor = getParamColor(reading.metricKey)

    val (statusIcon, statusColor) = when (reading.status.uppercase()) {
        "WARNING" -> Icons.Default.Warning to Color(0xFFFFB300)
        "ERROR" -> Icons.Default.Clear to Color(0xFFE53935)
        else -> Icons.Default.Check to Color(0xFF43A047)
    }

    Card(
        modifier = modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant.copy(alpha = 0.3f)),
        border = BorderStroke(2.dp, borderColor)
    ) {
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .padding(16.dp)
                .fillMaxWidth(),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
        ) {
            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                Text(
                    text = reading.label,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                )

                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))

                val minStr = reading.minValue?.let { it } ?: " — "
                val maxStr = reading.maxValue?.let { it } ?: " — "

                Text(
                    text = "$minStr  |  $maxStr",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                    color = Color.Gray
                )
            }

            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "${reading.value} ${reading.unit ?: ""}".trim(),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                )

                Icon(
                    imageVector = statusIcon,
                    contentDescription = reading.status,
                    tint = statusColor,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun PaginationControl(
    currentPage: Int,
    totalCount: Int,
    pageSize: Int = 8,
    onPageChanged: (Int) -> Unit
) {
    val maxPages = ceil(totalCount.toDouble() / pageSize).toInt().coerceAtLeast(1)

    Row(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .padding(vertical = 16.dp),
        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        TextButton(
            onClick = { onPageChanged(1) },
            enabled = currentPage > 1
        ) { Text("◀◀", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }

        TextButton(
            onClick = { onPageChanged(currentPage - 1) },
            enabled = currentPage > 1
        ) { Text("◀", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }

        Card(
            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primaryContainer),
            modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 8.dp)
        ) {
            Text(
                text = "$currentPage / $maxPages",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                modifier = Modifier.padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer
            )
        }

        TextButton(
            onClick = { onPageChanged(currentPage + 1) },
            enabled = currentPage < maxPages
        ) { Text("▶", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }

        TextButton(
            onClick = { onPageChanged(maxPages) },
            enabled = currentPage < maxPages
        ) { Text("▶▶", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold) }
    }
}

@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class, ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun DeviceListScreen(
    uiState: MainUiState,
    onDeviceSelected: (String) -> Unit
) {
    val groupedDevices = remember(uiState.devices) {
        uiState.devices.groupBy { it.model }
    }

    val expandedStates = remember {
        mutableStateMapOf<String, Boolean>().apply {
            groupedDevices.keys.forEach { model -> this[model] = true }
        }
    }

    Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.connected_devices),
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(16.dp),
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
        )

        LazyColumn(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            groupedDevices.forEach { (model, devices) ->
                val isExpanded = expandedStates[model] ?: true

                stickyHeader {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .background(MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant)
                            .clickable {
                                expandedStates[model] = !isExpanded
                            }
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = model,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelLarge,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Black,
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimary
                        )
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = if (isExpanded) Icons.Default.KeyboardArrowUp else Icons.Default.KeyboardArrowDown,
                            contentDescription = if (isExpanded) "Hide" else "Expand",
                            tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onPrimary
                        )
                    }
                }

                if (isExpanded) {
                    items(devices) { device ->
                        val isSelected = device.serialNumber == uiState.selectedDeviceId

                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .background(if (isSelected) MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primaryContainer else Color.Transparent)
                                .clickable { onDeviceSelected(device.serialNumber) }
                                .padding(16.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                                Text(
                                    text = device.name,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyLarge,
                                    fontWeight = if (isSelected) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = device.statusName,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall,
                                    color = Color.Gray
                                )
                            }
                            if (isSelected) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Check,
                                    contentDescription = "Checked",
                                    tint = colorScheme.onSurface
                                )
                            }
                        }
                        HorizontalDivider(color = colorScheme.outlineVariant.copy(alpha = 0.5f))
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun LoadingStateScreen() {
    Column(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center,
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        CircularProgressIndicator(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary)
    }
}

            color = Color.Gray,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
        )
        TextButton(
            onClick = { onRefresh() }
        ) {
            Text(
                text = stringResource(R.string.state_empty_refresh),
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary
            )
        }
    }
}

package com.rtsr.signal.screens

import androidx.compose.animation.Crossfade
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Arrangement

@Composable
fun SettingsScreen(
    uiState: MainUiState,
    onSaveClick: (String, String, String) -> Unit,
    onFieldsValueChange: (String, String, String) -> Unit,
    onCheckConnectionClick: (String, String, String) -> Unit,
    onBack: (AppScreen) -> Unit,
    screen: AppScreen
) {
    val colorScheme = MaterialTheme.colorScheme

    var httpsStatus by remember { mutableStateOf(uiState.useHttps) }
    val isChecked = httpsStatus == "s"

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(24.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.settings_title),
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge,
            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
            modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 32.dp, top = 16.dp)
        )

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
        ) {
            OutlinedTextField(
                value = uiState.apiAddress,
                onValueChange = { newAddress ->
                    onFieldsValueChange(newAddress, uiState.apiPort, uiState.useHttps)
                },
                label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.api_address_hint)) },
                modifier = Modifier.weight(0.7f),
                isError = uiState.addressErrorResId != null,
                keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                supportingText = {
                    uiState.addressErrorResId?.let { resId ->
                        Text(text = stringResource(resId), color = colorScheme.error)
                    }
                },
                enabled = !uiState.isConnectionChecking
            )

            OutlinedTextField(
                value = uiState.apiPort,
                onValueChange = { newPort ->
                    onFieldsValueChange(uiState.apiAddress, newPort, uiState.useHttps)
                },
                label = { Text(stringResource(R.string.port_hint)) },
                modifier = Modifier.weight(0.3f),
                isError = uiState.portErrorResId != null,
                keyboardOptions = KeyboardOptions(keyboardType = KeyboardType.Number),
                supportingText = {
                    uiState.portErrorResId?.let { resId ->
                        Text(text = stringResource(resId), color = colorScheme.error)
                    }
                },
                enabled = !uiState.isConnectionChecking
            )
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            Button(
                onClick = { onSaveClick(uiState.apiAddress, uiState.apiPort, httpsStatus) },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(0.5f)
                    .height(50.dp),
                enabled = uiState.connectionStatus == ConnectionStatus.SUCCESS && !uiState.isConnectionChecking
            ) {
                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(R.string.btn_save),
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                    )

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))

                    val icon = if (uiState.isAddressSaved) Icons.Default.Check else Icons.Default.Clear
                    val iconContentDescription = if (uiState.isAddressSaved) "Saved" else "Unsaved"

                    Icon(
                        imageVector = icon,
                        contentDescription = iconContentDescription,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                    )
                }
            }

            Button(
                onClick = { onCheckConnectionClick(uiState.apiAddress, uiState.apiPort, uiState.useHttps) },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .weight(0.5f)
                    .height(50.dp),
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                    containerColor = when (uiState.connectionStatus) {
                        ConnectionStatus.SUCCESS -> colorScheme.surfaceVariant
                        ConnectionStatus.FAILED -> colorScheme.errorContainer
                        ConnectionStatus.NOT_TESTED -> colorScheme.primary
                    }
                ),
                enabled = !uiState.isConnectionChecking
            ) {
                if (uiState.isConnectionChecking) {
                    CircularProgressIndicator(
                        modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp),
                        color = colorScheme.onPrimary,
                        strokeWidth = 2.dp
                    )
                } else {
                    Row(
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(4.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(R.string.btn_check_connection),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                        )
                        Crossfade(targetState = uiState.connectionStatus, label = "icon") { status ->
                            when (status) {
                                ConnectionStatus.SUCCESS -> Icon(Icons.Default.Check, contentDescription = "OK")
                                ConnectionStatus.FAILED -> Icon(Icons.Default.Clear, contentDescription = "Fail")
                                ConnectionStatus.NOT_TESTED -> Icon(Icons.Default.Warning, contentDescription = "Warning")
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

        Switch(
            checked = isChecked,
            onCheckedChange = { checked ->
                httpsStatus = if (checked) "s" else ""
                onFieldsValueChange(uiState.apiAddress, uiState.apiPort, httpsStatus)
            }
        )
        Text(
            text = stringResource(R.string.str_use_https),
            modifier = Modifier.padding(start = 8.dp)
        )

        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

        TextButton(
            onClick = { onBack(screen) },
            content = {
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(R.string.btn_back),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                )
            }
        )
    }
}

<file path="Signal.Andriod/app/src/main/java/com/rtsr/signal/services/ApiService.kt">
package com.rtsr.signal.services

import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.EquipmentDto
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.GraphPointDto
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.LatestReadingResponse
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.LoginRequest
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.PagedLogsDto
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.PingResponse
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.ReadingLogDto
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.RefreshRequest
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.TokenResponse
import com.rtsr.signal.models.MainUiState
import okhttp3.Authenticator
import okhttp3.Interceptor
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Response
import okhttp3.internal.tls.OkHostnameVerifier.verify
import retrofit2.Retrofit
import retrofit2.converter.gson.GsonConverterFactory
import retrofit2.http.Body
import retrofit2.http.GET
import retrofit2.http.Header
import retrofit2.http.POST
import retrofit2.http.Path
import retrofit2.http.Query
import retrofit2.http.Url
import javax.net.ssl.HostnameVerifier

interface ApiService {
    @POST("api/auth/login")
    suspend fun login(
        @Body request: LoginRequest,
        @Header("X-Device-Name") deviceName: String
    ): TokenResponse

    @POST("api/auth/refresh")
    fun refreshTokens(@Body request: RefreshRequest): retrofit2.Call<TokenResponse>

    @POST("api/auth/refresh")
    suspend fun refreshTokensAsync(@Body request: RefreshRequest): TokenResponse

    @POST("api/auth/logout")
    suspend fun logoutServer(@Body request: RefreshRequest): Any

    @GET("api/equipment/{serialNumber}/logs")
    suspend fun getEquipmentLogs(
        @Path("serialNumber") serialNumber: String,
        @Query("period") period: String,
        @Query("identifier") identifier: String?,
        @Query("page") page: Int,
        @Query("pageSize") pageSize: Int
    ): PagedLogsDto

    @GET("api/equipment")
    suspend fun getEquipmentList(): List<EquipmentDto>

    @GET
    suspend fun checkApiHealth(@Url url: String): PingResponse

    @GET("api/equipment/{serialNumber}/graph")
    suspend fun getFullGraphData(
        @Path("serialNumber") serialNumber: String,
        @Query("period") period: String
    ): List<GraphPointDto>

    @GET("api/equipment/{serialNumber}/latest")
    suspend fun getLatestReadings(
        @Path("serialNumber") serialNumber: String
    ): List<LatestReadingResponse>
}

object NetworkClient {
    private var apiService: ApiService? = null

    fun init(uiState: MainUiState, tokenManager: TokenManager?) {
        val savedAddress = uiState.apiAddress
        val savedPort = uiState.apiPort
        val savedProtocol = uiState.useHttps
        val dynamicBaseUrl = "http$savedProtocol://$savedAddress:$savedPort/"

        val baseOkHttpClient = OkHttpClient
            .Builder()
            .build()

        val baseRetrofit = Retrofit.Builder()
            .baseUrl(dynamicBaseUrl)
            .client(baseOkHttpClient)
            .addConverterFactory(GsonConverterFactory.create())
            .build()

        val authServiceForRefresh = baseRetrofit.create(ApiService::class.java)

        val authInterceptor = Interceptor { chain ->
            val requestBuilder = chain.request().newBuilder()
            tokenManager?.getAccessToken()?.let { token ->
                requestBuilder.addHeader("Authorization", "Bearer $token")
            }
            chain.proceed(requestBuilder.build())
        }

        val tokenAuthenticator = Authenticator { _, response ->
            if (response.request.url.encodedPath.contains("api/auth/login")) {
                return@Authenticator null
            }

            if (response.request.url.encodedPath.contains("api/auth/refresh")) {
                return@Authenticator null
            }

            if (response.responseCount > 1) {
                tokenManager?.clearTokens()
                return@Authenticator null
            }

            val refreshToken = tokenManager?.getRefreshToken() ?: return@Authenticator null

            synchronized(this) {
                try {
                    val refreshResponse = authServiceForRefresh.refreshTokens(RefreshRequest(refreshToken)).execute()

                    if (refreshResponse.isSuccessful && refreshResponse.body() != null) {
                        val newTokens = refreshResponse.body()!!
                        tokenManager.saveTokens(newTokens.accessToken, newTokens.refreshToken)

                        return@Authenticator response.request.newBuilder()
                            .header("Authorization", "Bearer ${newTokens.accessToken}")
                            .build()
                    } else {
                        tokenManager.clearTokens()
                        return@Authenticator null
                    }
                } catch (_: Exception) {
                    return@Authenticator null
                }
            }
        }

        val protectedOkHttpClient = OkHttpClient.Builder()
            .addInterceptor(authInterceptor)
            .authenticator(tokenAuthenticator)
            .build()

        val retrofit = Retrofit.Builder()
            .baseUrl(dynamicBaseUrl)
            .client(protectedOkHttpClient)
            .addConverterFactory(GsonConverterFactory.create())
            .build()

        synchronized(this) {
            apiService = retrofit.create(ApiService::class.java)
        }
    }

    fun getApi(): ApiService {
        return apiService ?: synchronized(this) {
            apiService ?: throw UninitializedPropertyAccessException("NetworkClient не был инициализирован. Вызовите init() перед использованием.")
        }
    }
}

private val Response.responseCount: Int
    get() = generateSequence(this) { it.priorResponse }.count()
</file>

<file path="Signal.Andriod/app/src/main/java/com/rtsr/signal/services/TokenManager.kt">
package com.rtsr.signal.services

import android.content.Context
import androidx.security.crypto.EncryptedSharedPreferences
import androidx.security.crypto.MasterKey
import androidx.core.content.edit
import java.io.File

class TokenManager(context: Context) {
    private val appContext = context.applicationContext
    private val fileName = "secret_shared_prefs"

    private val prefs by lazy {
        try {
            createEncryptedPrefs()
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            deleteAllEncryptedFiles()
            createEncryptedPrefs()
        }
    }

    private fun createEncryptedPrefs() = EncryptedSharedPreferences.create(
        appContext,
        fileName,
        MasterKey.Builder(appContext).setKeyScheme(MasterKey.KeyScheme.AES256_GCM).build(),
        EncryptedSharedPreferences.PrefKeyEncryptionScheme.AES256_SIV,
        EncryptedSharedPreferences.PrefValueEncryptionScheme.AES256_GCM
    )

    private fun deleteAllEncryptedFiles() {
        try {
            val sharedPrefsDir = File(appContext.filesDir.parent, "shared_prefs")
            if (sharedPrefsDir.exists() && sharedPrefsDir.isDirectory) {
                File(sharedPrefsDir, "$fileName.xml").delete()

                sharedPrefsDir.listFiles()?.forEach { file ->
                    if (file.name.contains("androidx_security_crypto")) {
                        file.delete()
                    }
                }
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    fun getAccessToken(): String? = prefs.getString("access_token", null)

    fun getRefreshToken(): String? = prefs.getString("refresh_token", null)

    fun saveTokens(accessToken: String?, refreshToken: String?) {
        prefs.edit {
            accessToken?.let { putString("access_token", it) }
            refreshToken?.let { putString("refresh_token", it) }
        }
    }

    fun clearTokens() {
        prefs.edit {
            remove("access_token")
            remove("refresh_token")
        }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.Andriod/app/src/main/java/com/rtsr/signal/ui/theme/Theme.kt">
package com.rtsr.signal.ui.theme

import android.app.Activity
import android.os.Build
import androidx.compose.foundation.isSystemInDarkTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.darkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.dynamicDarkColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.dynamicLightColorScheme
import androidx.compose.material3.lightColorScheme
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.SideEffect
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.toArgb
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalView
import androidx.core.view.WindowCompat

private val DarkColorScheme = darkColorScheme(
    primary = SignalPurple,
    background = DarkBackground,
    surface = DarkSurface,
    onPrimary = LightBackground,
    onBackground = DarkOnBackground,
    onSurface = DarkOnBackground,
    surfaceVariant = DarkSurface
)

private val LightColorScheme = lightColorScheme(
    primary = SignalPurple,
    background = LightBackground,
    surface = LightSurface,
    onPrimary = LightBackground,
    onBackground = LightOnBackground,
    onSurface = LightOnBackground,
    surfaceVariant = SignalPurple
)

@Composable
fun SignalTheme(
    darkTheme: Boolean = isSystemInDarkTheme(),
    dynamicColor: Boolean = true,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val colorScheme = if (darkTheme) DarkColorScheme else LightColorScheme
    val view = LocalView.current

    if (!view.isInEditMode) {
        SideEffect {
            val window = (view.context as Activity).window
            window.statusBarColor = if (darkTheme) DarkBackground.toArgb() else SignalPurple.toArgb()

            WindowCompat.getInsetsController(window, view).isAppearanceLightStatusBars = false
        }
    }

    MaterialTheme(
        colorScheme = colorScheme,
        typography = Typography,
        content = content
    )
}
