<file path="Signal.Andriod/app/src/main/java/com/rtsr/signal/viewmodel/AppViewModel.kt">
package com.rtsr.signal.viewmodel

import android.app.Application
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.update
import android.content.Context
import android.os.Build
import android.util.Log
import androidx.annotation.RequiresApi
import androidx.compose.ui.graphics.Color
import com.rtsr.signal.models.MainUiState
import com.rtsr.signal.models.PeriodType
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import java.time.LocalDateTime
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import androidx.core.content.edit
import androidx.lifecycle.AndroidViewModel
import androidx.lifecycle.viewModelScope
import com.rtsr.signal.R
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.LoginRequest
import com.rtsr.signal.dtos.RefreshRequest
import com.rtsr.signal.models.AppScreen
import com.rtsr.signal.models.ConnectionStatus
import com.rtsr.signal.models.SignalApplication
import com.rtsr.signal.models.ViewMode
import com.rtsr.signal.services.NetworkClient
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

@RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
class AppViewModel(application: Application) : AndroidViewModel(application) {
    private val sharedPrefs = application.getSharedPreferences("app_prefs", Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
    private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow(MainUiState())
    val uiState: StateFlow<MainUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()

    val savedAddress = sharedPrefs.getString("api_address", "127.0.0.1") ?: "127.0.0.1"
    val savedPort = sharedPrefs.getString("api_port", "5000") ?: "5000"

    val savedHttpsStatus = sharedPrefs.getString("api_protocol", "") ?: ""

    val api get() = NetworkClient.getApi()

    val tokenManager = SignalApplication.tokenManager

    init {
        loadConnectionSettings(savedAddress, savedPort, savedHttpsStatus)

        tryAutoLogin()
    }

    fun updateClient(){
        NetworkClient.init(_uiState.value, tokenManager)
    }

    fun loadDataForCurrentView() {
        when (_uiState.value.viewMode) {
            ViewMode.LATEST ->  loadLatestReadings()
            ViewMode.GRAPH -> loadGraphData()
            ViewMode.TABLE -> loadDeviceData()
        }
    }

    fun loadAllData() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoading = true, isConnected = true) }
            try {
                val remoteDevices = api.getEquipmentList()
                val firstDeviceId = remoteDevices.firstOrNull()?.serialNumber
                val currentSelectedId = _uiState.value.selectedDeviceId ?: sharedPrefs.getString("selected_device_id", firstDeviceId)

                _uiState.update { currentState ->
                    currentState.copy(
                        devices = remoteDevices,
                        selectedDeviceId = currentSelectedId,
                        currentPage = 1
                    )
                }

                if (currentSelectedId != null) {
                    try {
                        when (_uiState.value.viewMode){
                            ViewMode.GRAPH -> {
                                _uiState.update { it.copy(isGraphLoading = true) }
                                val points = api.getFullGraphData(currentSelectedId, _uiState.value.selectedPeriod.name)

                                val response = api.getEquipmentLogs(
                                    currentSelectedId,
                                    _uiState.value.selectedPeriod.name,
                                    _uiState.value.selectedParamIdentifier,
                                    1,
                                    8
                                )

                                _uiState.update { currentState ->
                                    currentState.copy(
                                        isLoading = false,
                                        isGraphLoading = false,
                                        graphPoints = points,
                                        availableParams = response.availableIdentifiers,
                                        lastUpdateTime = getCurrentFormattedTime(),
                                    )
                                }

                                setFirstParam()
                            }
                            ViewMode.TABLE -> {
                                val response = api.getEquipmentLogs(
                                    currentSelectedId,
                                    _uiState.value.selectedPeriod.name,
                                    _uiState.value.selectedParamIdentifier,
                                    1,
                                    8
                                )
                                _uiState.update { currentState ->
                                    currentState.copy(
                                        isLoading = false,
                                        logsList = response.logs,
                                        availableParams = response.availableIdentifiers,
                                        selectedParamIdentifier = null,
                                        totalCount = response.totalCount,
                                        lastUpdateTime = getCurrentFormattedTime()
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                            ViewMode.LATEST -> {
                                val response = api.getEquipmentLogs(
                                    currentSelectedId,
                                    _uiState.value.selectedPeriod.name,
                                    null,
                                    1,
                                    8
                                )
                                val readingsResponse = api.getLatestReadings(currentSelectedId)

                                _uiState.update { currentState ->
                                    currentState.copy(
                                        isLoading = false,
                                        logsList = response.logs,
                                        availableParams = response.availableIdentifiers,
                                        selectedParamIdentifier = null,
                                        totalCount = response.totalCount,
                                        lastUpdateTime = getCurrentFormattedTime(),
                                        latestReadings = readingsResponse,
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    } catch (_: Exception) {
                        _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoading = false, isConnected = false, isGraphLoading = false, logsList = emptyList(), totalCount = 0) }
                    }
                } else {
                    _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoading = false, logsList = emptyList(), totalCount = 0) }
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoading = false, isConnected = false) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun setFirstParam(){
        _uiState.update { currentState ->
            currentState.copy(
                selectedParamIdentifier = _uiState.value.selectedParamIdentifier ?: _uiState.value.availableParams.firstOrNull(),
                currentPage = 1
            )
        }
    }

    fun loadLatestReadings(){
        val deviceId = _uiState.value.selectedDeviceId ?: return
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val readingsResponse = api.getLatestReadings(deviceId)
                _uiState.update { currentState ->
                    currentState.copy(
                        isLoading = false,
                        latestReadings = readingsResponse
                    )
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoading = false) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun loadDeviceData() {
        val deviceId = _uiState.value.selectedDeviceId ?: return
        val period = _uiState.value.selectedPeriod.name
        val param = _uiState.value.selectedParamIdentifier
        val page = _uiState.value.currentPage
        val pageSize = 8

        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val response = api.getEquipmentLogs(deviceId, period, param, page, pageSize)
                _uiState.update { currentState ->
                    currentState.copy(
                        isLoading = false,
                        isPageLoading = false,
                        logsList = response.logs,
                        availableParams = response.availableIdentifiers,
                        totalCount = response.totalCount
                    )
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoading = false, isPageLoading = false) }
            }
        }
    }

    fun loadGraphData() {
        val deviceId = _uiState.value.selectedDeviceId ?: return
        val period = _uiState.value.selectedPeriod.name

        viewModelScope.launch {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(isGraphLoading = true) }
            try {
                val points = api.getFullGraphData(deviceId, period).onEach { point -> if(point.value == "n/a") point.value = null }

                _uiState.update { currentState ->
                    currentState.copy(
                        isGraphLoading = false,
                        graphPoints = points
                    )
                }

                setFirstParam()
            } catch (_: Exception) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isGraphLoading = false) }
            }
        }
    }

    @RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
    fun selectDevice(id: String) {
        sharedPrefs.edit {
            putString("selected_device_id", id)
        }

        _uiState.update { currentState ->
            currentState.copy(
                selectedDeviceId = id,
                currentPage = 1
            )
        }

        loadAllData()
    }

    fun refreshData() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(isRefreshing = true)

            loadAllData()

            _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
                isRefreshing = false
            )
        }
    }

    private fun tryAutoLogin() {
        val refreshToken = tokenManager.getRefreshToken()
        if (refreshToken.isNullOrBlank()) {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(isAutoLoginChecking = false, isAuthorized = false) }
            return
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                val response = api.refreshTokensAsync(RefreshRequest(refreshToken))
                tokenManager.saveTokens(response.accessToken, response.refreshToken)
                val fullName = listOfNotNull(response.lastName, response.firstName, response.middleName)
                    .joinToString(" ")
                    .ifBlank { " " }

                _uiState.update { it.copy(isAuthorized = true, userFullName = fullName) }
                loadAllData()
            } catch (e: retrofit2.HttpException) {
                tokenManager.clearTokens()
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isAuthorized = false) }
            } catch (e: java.io.IOException) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isAuthorized = false) }
            } finally {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isAutoLoginChecking = false) }
            }
        }
    }

    suspend fun login(loginText: String, passwordText: String) {
        _uiState.update { it.copy(loginErrorMessage = null,isLoginLoading = true) }

        if (loginText.isNotBlank() && passwordText.isNotBlank()) {
            try {
                val response = api.login(LoginRequest(loginText, passwordText), getDeviceName())
                tokenManager.saveTokens(response.accessToken, response.refreshToken)

                val fullName = listOfNotNull(response.lastName, response.firstName, response.middleName)
                    .joinToString(" ")
                    .ifBlank { " " }

                _uiState.update { it.copy(
                    isAuthorized = true,
                    userFullName = fullName,
                    currentScreen = AppScreen.MONITORING
                ) }
                loadAllData()
            } catch (_: retrofit2.HttpException) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(loginErrorMessage = R.string.error_login_failed) }
            } catch (_: java.io.IOException) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(loginErrorMessage = R.string.error_api_no_response) }
            } finally {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isLoginLoading = false) }
            }
        } else {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(loginErrorMessage = R.string.error_empty_fields, isLoginLoading = false) }
        }
    }

    fun loadConnectionSettings(address: String, port: String, httpsStatus: String){
        _uiState.value = _uiState.value.copy(
            apiAddress = address,
            apiPort = port,
            useHttps = httpsStatus
        )
        updateClient()
    }

    fun saveSettings(address: String, port: String, httpsStatus: String) {
        val isAddressValid = validateAddress(address)
        val isPortValid = validatePort(port)
        if (!isAddressValid || !isPortValid) {

            _uiState.update { currentState ->
                currentState.copy(
                    addressErrorResId = if (isAddressValid) null else R.string.error_invalid_address,
                    portErrorResId = if (isPortValid) null else R.string.error_invalid_port
                )
            }

            return
        }

        sharedPrefs.edit {
            putString("api_address", address.trim())
                .putString("api_port", port.trim())
                .putString("api_protocol", httpsStatus)
        }

        loadConnectionSettings(address, port, httpsStatus)

        _uiState.update { currentState ->
            currentState.copy(
                addressErrorResId = null,
                portErrorResId = null,
                isAddressSaved = true
            )
        }
    }

    fun onSettingsInputChanged(inputAddress: String, inputPort: String, httpsStatus: String) {
        _uiState.update { it.copy(
            apiAddress = inputAddress,
            apiPort = inputPort,
            useHttps = httpsStatus
        )}

        if (inputAddress.trim() == savedAddress && inputPort.trim() == savedPort) {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(
                connectionStatus = ConnectionStatus.SUCCESS,
                isAddressSaved = true
            )}
        } else {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(
                connectionStatus = ConnectionStatus.NOT_TESTED,
                isAddressSaved = false
            )}
        }
    }

    fun checkConnection(address: String, port: String, protocol: String) {
        val isAddressValid = validateAddress(address)
        val isPortValid = validatePort(port)

        if (!isAddressValid || !isPortValid) {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(
                addressErrorResId = if (isAddressValid) null else R.string.error_invalid_address,
                portErrorResId = if (isPortValid) null else R.string.error_invalid_port,
                connectionStatus = ConnectionStatus.FAILED
            ) }
            return
        }

        viewModelScope.launch {
            _uiState.update { it.copy(isConnectionChecking = true, apiAddress = address, apiPort = port) }
            try {
                val testUrl = "http$protocol://${address.trim()}:${port.trim()}/api/ping"

                val response = api.checkApiHealth(testUrl)

                if (response.service == "SignalTrack.Api") {
                    _uiState.update { it.copy(isConnectionChecking = false, connectionStatus = ConnectionStatus.SUCCESS) }
                } else {
                    _uiState.update { it.copy(isConnectionChecking = false, connectionStatus = ConnectionStatus.FAILED) }
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                _uiState.update { it.copy(isConnectionChecking = false, connectionStatus = ConnectionStatus.FAILED) }
            }
        }
    }

    private fun validateAddress(address: String): Boolean{
        val ipWithDomainRegex = Regex("^(?:(?:25[0-5]|2[0-4][0-9]|[01]?[0-9][0-9]?)\\.){3}(?:25[0-5]|2[0-4][0-9]|[01]?[0-9][0-9]?)\$|^(?:[a-zA-Z0-9](?:[a-zA-Z0-9-]{0,61}[a-zA-Z0-9])?\\.)+[a-zA-Z]{2,6}\$")
        return address.trim().matches(ipWithDomainRegex)
    }

    private fun validatePort(port: String): Boolean{
        val portInt = port.trim().toIntOrNull()
        return portInt != null && portInt in 1..65535
    }

    fun navigateTo(screen: AppScreen) {
        val isSettingsScreen = screen == AppScreen.SETTINGS
        _uiState.update { currentState ->
            currentState.copy(
                currentScreen = screen,
                addressErrorResId = if (isSettingsScreen) null else currentState.addressErrorResId,
                portErrorResId = if (isSettingsScreen) null else currentState.portErrorResId,
                connectionStatus = if (isSettingsScreen &&
                    currentState.apiAddress == savedAddress &&
                    currentState.apiPort == savedPort) {
                    ConnectionStatus.SUCCESS
                } else {
                    ConnectionStatus.NOT_TESTED
                }
            )
        }
    }

    fun logout() {
        val refreshToken = tokenManager.getRefreshToken()

        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                if (!refreshToken.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    NetworkClient.getApi().logoutServer(RefreshRequest(refreshToken))
                }
            } catch (_: Exception) {}
            finally {
                tokenManager.clearTokens()

                sharedPrefs.edit(commit = true) {
                    remove("selected_device_id")
                }

                _uiState.update { currentState ->
                    currentState.copy(
                        isAuthorized = false,
                        selectedDeviceId = null,
                        userFullName = " ",
                        currentPage = 1
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun selectPeriod(period: PeriodType) {
        _uiState.update { it.copy(
            selectedPeriod = period,
            currentPage = 1)
        }
        loadDataForCurrentView()
    }

    fun selectParamIdentifier(identifier: String?) {
        _uiState.update {
            it.copy(selectedParamIdentifier = identifier,
                currentPage = 1)
        }
        loadDataForCurrentView()
    }

    fun changePage(page: Int) {
        if (page < 1 || _uiState.value.isPageLoading || _uiState.value.isLoading) return
        _uiState.update { it.copy(currentPage = page, isPageLoading = true) }
        loadDeviceData()
    }

    fun toggleViewMode(newMode: ViewMode) {
        _uiState.update { it.copy(viewMode = newMode) }
        loadDataForCurrentView()
    }

    @RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
    private fun getCurrentFormattedTime(): String {
        return LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("dd.MM.yyyy HH:mm:ss"))
    }

    fun getParamColor(paramId: String): Color {
        val hash = paramId.hashCode()
        return Color(
            red = (hash and 0xFF0000 shr 16) / 255f * 0.6f + 0.4f,
            green = (hash and 0x00FF00 shr 8) / 255f * 0.6f + 0.4f,
            blue = (hash and 0x0000FF) / 255f * 0.6f + 0.4f,
            alpha = 1f
        )
    }

    fun getDeviceName(): String {
        val manufacturer = Build.MANUFACTURER
        val model = Build.MODEL
        return if (model.startsWith(manufacturer)) {
            model
        } else {
            "$manufacturer $model"
        }
    }

}
</file>

package com.rtsr.signal

import android.os.Build
import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.activity.viewModels
import androidx.annotation.RequiresApi
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.material3.MaterialTheme
import androidx.compose.material3.Surface
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.lifecycle.ViewModelProvider
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.compose.viewModel
import androidx.navigation.compose.NavHost
import androidx.navigation.compose.composable
import androidx.navigation.compose.rememberNavController
import com.rtsr.signal.screens.AppNavigationScreen
import com.rtsr.signal.screens.MainNavigationContainer
import com.rtsr.signal.ui.theme.SignalTheme
import com.rtsr.signal.viewmodel.AppViewModel

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    private val appViewModel: AppViewModel by viewModels()

    @RequiresApi(Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()
        setContent {
            SignalTheme {
                Surface(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.background
                ) {
                    AppNavigationScreen(appViewModel)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

</data-extraction-rules>

<file path="Signal.Andriod/app/src/main/res/gemini-code-1793523594955.py">
import os
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path

# Определяем базовые пути относительно директории res
RES_DIR = Path.cwd()
SRC_MAIN_DIR = RES_DIR.parent
KOTLIN_DIR = SRC_MAIN_DIR / "kotlin"
JAVA_DIR = SRC_MAIN_DIR / "java"

OUTPUT_FILE = RES_DIR / "design_analysis_report.txt"

def get_xml_colors():
    """Сбор цветов из values/colors.xml и themes.xml"""
    colors = {}
    values_dir = RES_DIR / "values"
    if not values_dir.exists():
        return colors

    for xml_file in values_dir.glob("*.xml"):
        try:
            tree = ET.parse(xml_file)
            root = tree.getroot()
            for elem in root:
                if elem.tag == "color" and "name" in elem.attrib:
                    colors[elem.attrib["name"]] = elem.text.strip() if elem.text else ""
        except Exception:
            continue
    return colors

def get_compose_colors(code_files):
    """Поиск объявлений цветов в Compose (Color(0x...))"""
    colors = set()
    pattern = re.compile(r"(val|var)\s+([A-Za-z0-9_]+)\s*=\s*(Color\(0x[0-9a-fA-F]+\))")
    for f in code_files:
        try:
            content = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
            for match in pattern.finditer(content):
                colors.add(f"{match.group(2)} = {match.group(3)}")
        except Exception:
            continue
    return sorted(colors)

def get_fonts(code_files):
    """Сбор шрифтов из res/font и определений в коде"""
    fonts = {
        "files": [],
        "compose_references": set()
    }
    
    font_dir = RES_DIR / "font"
    if font_dir.exists():
        for f in font_dir.glob("*"):
            if f.is_file():
                fonts["files"].append(f.name)
                
    # Поиск FontFamily в Compose (Type.kt)
    pattern_compose = re.compile(r"FontFamily\s*\((.*?)\)", re.DOTALL)
    for f in code_files:
        try:
            content = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
            matches = pattern_compose.findall(content)
            for m in matches:
                clean_ref = " ".join(m.split())
                if clean_ref:
                    fonts["compose_references"].add(clean_ref)
        except Exception:
            continue
    return fonts

def get_icons(code_files):
    """Сбор drawable-ресурсов и вызовов Icons.* в Compose"""
    drawables = []
    compose_icons = set()

    # Векторные и растровые иконки в drawable
    for d_path in RES_DIR.glob("drawable*"):
        if d_path.is_dir():
            for f in d_path.glob("*.xml"):
                drawables.append(f.name)

    # Поиск Compose Material Icons (включая Icons.Filled, Icons.Outlined, Icons.Default и др.)
    icon_pattern = re.compile(r"Icons\.(Filled|Outlined|Rounded|Sharp|TwoTone|Default)\.([A-Za-z0-9_]+)")
    
    for f in code_files:
        try:
            content = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
            for match in icon_pattern.finditer(content):
                compose_icons.add(f"Icons.{match.group(1)}.{match.group(2)}")
        except Exception:
            continue

    return sorted(set(drawables)), sorted(compose_icons)

def main():
    # Находим все файлы с исходным кодом Kotlin / Java
    code_files = []
    for directory in [KOTLIN_DIR, JAVA_DIR]:
        if directory.exists():
            code_files.extend(list(directory.rglob("*.kt")))
            code_files.extend(list(directory.rglob("*.java")))

    xml_colors = get_xml_colors()
    compose_colors = get_compose_colors(code_files)
    fonts = get_fonts(code_files)
    drawables, compose_icons = get_icons(code_files)

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        out.write("==============================\n")
        out.write(" ДИЗАЙН-ЭЛЕМЕНТЫ ПРИЛОЖЕНИЯ \n")
        out.write("==============================\n\n")

        # 1. Цвета
        out.write("--- 1. ЦВЕТА ---\n")
        out.write("[XML Colors (values/colors.xml)]:\n")
        if xml_colors:
            for name, val in xml_colors.items():
                out.write(f"  - @color/{name}: {val}\n")
        else:
            out.write("  (Цвета в XML не найдены)\n")

        out.write("\n[Jetpack Compose Colors]:\n")
        if compose_colors:
            for c in compose_colors:
                out.write(f"  - {c}\n")
        else:
            out.write("  (Определения Compose Color() не найдены)\n")

        # 2. Шрифты
        out.write("\n--- 2. ШРИФТЫ ---\n")
        out.write("[Файлы шрифтов в res/font/]:\n")
        if fonts["files"]:
            for f in fonts["files"]:
                out.write(f"  - {f}\n")
        else:
            out.write("  (Шрифты в res/font/ отсутствуют)\n")

        out.write("\n[Семейства шрифтов в Compose/Kotlin]:\n")
        if fonts["compose_references"]:
            for ref in sorted(fonts["compose_references"]):
                out.write(f"  - FontFamily: {ref}\n")
        else:
            out.write("  (Кастомные FontFamily в коде не обнаружены)\n")

        # 3. Иконки
        out.write("\n--- 3. ИКОНКИ ---\n")
        out.write("[Compose Material Icons (из material.icons.extended)]:\n")
        if compose_icons:
            for icon in compose_icons:
                out.write(f"  - {icon}\n")
        else:
            out.write("  (Вызовы Icons.* в коде не найдены)\n")

        out.write(f"\n[Локальные Drawable ресурсы (всего {len(drawables)})]:\n")
        if drawables:
            for d in drawables:
                out.write(f"  - {d}\n")
        else:
            out.write("  (Файлы в папках drawable не найдены)\n")

    print(f"Готово! Результаты сохранены в файл:\n{OUTPUT_FILE}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
</file>

<file path="Signal.Andriod/gradlew">
#!/bin/sh

#
# Copyright © 2015 the original authors.
#
# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the "License");
# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.
# You may obtain a copy of the License at
#
#      https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0
#
# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software
# distributed under the License is distributed on an "AS IS" BASIS,
# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.
# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and
# limitations under the License.
#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#

##############################################################################
#
#   Gradle start up script for POSIX generated by Gradle.
#
#   Important for running:
#
#   (1) You need a POSIX-compliant shell to run this script. If your /bin/sh is
#       noncompliant, but you have some other compliant shell such as ksh or
#       bash, then to run this script, type that shell name before the whole
#       command line, like:
#
#           ksh Gradle
#
#       Busybox and similar reduced shells will NOT work, because this script
#       requires all of these POSIX shell features:
#         * functions;
#         * expansions «$var», «${var}», «${var:-default}», «${var+SET}»,
#           «${var#prefix}», «${var%suffix}», and «$( cmd )»;
#         * compound commands having a testable exit status, especially «case»;
#         * various built-in commands including «command», «set», and «ulimit».
#
#   Important for patching:
#
#   (2) This script targets any POSIX shell, so it avoids extensions provided
#       by Bash, Ksh, etc; in particular arrays are avoided.
#
#       The "traditional" practice of packing multiple parameters into a
#       space-separated string is a well documented source of bugs and security
#       problems, so this is (mostly) avoided, by progressively accumulating
#       options in "$@", and eventually passing that to Java.
#
#       Where the inherited environment variables (DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS,
#       and GRADLE_OPTS) rely on word-splitting, this is performed explicitly;
#       see the in-line comments for details.
#
#       There are tweaks for specific operating systems such as AIX, CygWin,
#       Darwin, MinGW, and NonStop.
#
#   (3) This script is generated from the Groovy template
#       https://github.com/gradle/gradle/blob/HEAD/platforms/jvm/plugins-application/src/main/resources/org/gradle/api/internal/plugins/unixStartScript.txt
#       within the Gradle project.
#
#       You can find Gradle at https://github.com/gradle/gradle/.
#
##############################################################################

# Attempt to set APP_HOME

# Resolve links: $0 may be a link
app_path=$0

# Need this for daisy-chained symlinks.
while
    APP_HOME=${app_path%"${app_path##*/}"}  # leaves a trailing /; empty if no leading path
    [ -h "$app_path" ]
do
    ls=$( ls -ld "$app_path" )
    link=${ls#*' -> '}
    case $link in             #(
      /*)   app_path=$link ;; #(
      *)    app_path=$APP_HOME$link ;;
    esac
done

# This is normally unused
# shellcheck disable=SC2034
APP_BASE_NAME=${0##*/}
# Discard cd standard output in case $CDPATH is set (https://github.com/gradle/gradle/issues/25036)
APP_HOME=$( cd -P "${APP_HOME:-./}" > /dev/null && printf '%s\n' "$PWD" ) || exit

# Use the maximum available, or set MAX_FD != -1 to use that value.
MAX_FD=maximum

warn () {
    echo "$*"
} >&2

die () {
    echo
    echo "$*"
    echo
    exit 1
} >&2

# OS specific support (must be 'true' or 'false').
cygwin=false
msys=false
darwin=false
nonstop=false
case "$( uname )" in                #(
  CYGWIN* )         cygwin=true  ;; #(
  Darwin* )         darwin=true  ;; #(
  MSYS* | MINGW* )  msys=true    ;; #(
  NONSTOP* )        nonstop=true ;;
esac

CLASSPATH="\\\"\\\""


# Determine the Java command to use to start the JVM.
if [ -n "$JAVA_HOME" ] ; then
    if [ -x "$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java" ] ; then
        # IBM's JDK on AIX uses strange locations for the executables
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/jre/sh/java
    else
        JAVACMD=$JAVA_HOME/bin/java
    fi
    if [ ! -x "$JAVACMD" ] ; then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: $JAVA_HOME

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
else
    JAVACMD=java
    if ! command -v java >/dev/null 2>&1
    then
        die "ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH.

Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the
location of your Java installation."
    fi
fi

# Increase the maximum file descriptors if we can.
if ! "$cygwin" && ! "$darwin" && ! "$nonstop" ; then
    case $MAX_FD in #(
      max*)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -H is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        MAX_FD=$( ulimit -H -n ) ||
            warn "Could not query maximum file descriptor limit"
    esac
    case $MAX_FD in  #(
      '' | soft) :;; #(
      *)
        # In POSIX sh, ulimit -n is undefined. That's why the result is checked to see if it worked.
        # shellcheck disable=SC2039,SC3045
        ulimit -n "$MAX_FD" ||
            warn "Could not set maximum file descriptor limit to $MAX_FD"
    esac
fi

# Collect all arguments for the java command, stacking in reverse order:
#   * args from the command line
#   * the main class name
#   * -classpath
#   * -D...appname settings
#   * --module-path (only if needed)
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and GRADLE_OPTS environment variables.

# For Cygwin or MSYS, switch paths to Windows format before running java
if "$cygwin" || "$msys" ; then
    APP_HOME=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$APP_HOME" )
    CLASSPATH=$( cygpath --path --mixed "$CLASSPATH" )

    JAVACMD=$( cygpath --unix "$JAVACMD" )

    # Now convert the arguments - kludge to limit ourselves to /bin/sh
    for arg do
        if
            case $arg in                                #(
              -*)   false ;;                            # don't mess with options #(
              /?*)  t=${arg#/} t=/${t%%/*}              # looks like a POSIX filepath
                    [ -e "$t" ] ;;                      #(
              *)    false ;;
            esac
        then
            arg=$( cygpath --path --ignore --mixed "$arg" )
        fi
        # Roll the args list around exactly as many times as the number of
        # args, so each arg winds up back in the position where it started, but
        # possibly modified.
        #
        # NB: a `for` loop captures its iteration list before it begins, so
        # changing the positional parameters here affects neither the number of
        # iterations, nor the values presented in `arg`.
        shift                   # remove old arg
        set -- "$@" "$arg"      # push replacement arg
    done
fi


# Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS='"-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"'

# Collect all arguments for the java command:
#   * DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS, JAVA_OPTS, and optsEnvironmentVar are not allowed to contain shell fragments,
#     and any embedded shellness will be escaped.
#   * For example: A user cannot expect ${Hostname} to be expanded, as it is an environment variable and will be
#     treated as '${Hostname}' itself on the command line.

set -- \
        "-Dorg.gradle.appname=$APP_BASE_NAME" \
        -classpath "$CLASSPATH" \
        -jar "$APP_HOME/gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.jar" \
        "$@"

# Stop when "xargs" is not available.
if ! command -v xargs >/dev/null 2>&1
then
    die "xargs is not available"
fi

# Use "xargs" to parse quoted args.
#
# With -n1 it outputs one arg per line, with the quotes and backslashes removed.
#
# In Bash we could simply go:
#
#   readarray ARGS < <( xargs -n1 <<<"$var" ) &&
#   set -- "${ARGS[@]}" "$@"
#
# but POSIX shell has neither arrays nor command substitution, so instead we
# post-process each arg (as a line of input to sed) to backslash-escape any
# character that might be a shell metacharacter, then use eval to reverse
# that process (while maintaining the separation between arguments), and wrap
# the whole thing up as a single "set" statement.
#
# This will of course break if any of these variables contains a newline or
# an unmatched quote.
#

eval "set -- $(
        printf '%s\n' "$DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS $JAVA_OPTS $GRADLE_OPTS" |
        xargs -n1 |
        sed ' s~[^-[:alnum:]+,./:=@_]~\\&~g; ' |
        tr '\n' ' '
    )" '"$@"'

exec "$JAVACMD" "$@"
</file>

@if "%DEBUG%"=="" @echo off
@rem ##########################################################################
@rem
@rem  Gradle startup script for Windows
@rem
@rem ##########################################################################

@rem Set local scope for the variables with windows NT shell
if "%OS%"=="Windows_NT" setlocal

set DIRNAME=%~dp0
if "%DIRNAME%"=="" set DIRNAME=.
@rem This is normally unused
set APP_BASE_NAME=%~n0
set APP_HOME=%DIRNAME%

@rem Resolve any "." and ".." in APP_HOME to make it shorter.
for %%i in ("%APP_HOME%") do set APP_HOME=%%~fi

@rem Add default JVM options here. You can also use JAVA_OPTS and GRADLE_OPTS to pass JVM options to this script.
set DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS="-Xmx64m" "-Xms64m"

@rem Find java.exe
if defined JAVA_HOME goto findJavaFromJavaHome

set JAVA_EXE=java.exe
%JAVA_EXE% -version >NUL 2>&1
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto execute

echo. 1>&2
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is not set and no 'java' command could be found in your PATH. 1>&2
echo. 1>&2
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the 1>&2
echo location of your Java installation. 1>&2

goto fail

:findJavaFromJavaHome
set JAVA_HOME=%JAVA_HOME:"=%
set JAVA_EXE=%JAVA_HOME%/bin/java.exe

if exist "%JAVA_EXE%" goto execute

echo. 1>&2
echo ERROR: JAVA_HOME is set to an invalid directory: %JAVA_HOME% 1>&2
echo. 1>&2
echo Please set the JAVA_HOME variable in your environment to match the 1>&2
echo location of your Java installation. 1>&2

goto fail

:execute
@rem Setup the command line

set CLASSPATH=


@rem Execute Gradle
"%JAVA_EXE%" %DEFAULT_JVM_OPTS% %JAVA_OPTS% %GRADLE_OPTS% "-Dorg.gradle.appname=%APP_BASE_NAME%" -classpath "%CLASSPATH%" -jar "%APP_HOME%\gradle\wrapper\gradle-wrapper.jar" %*

:end
@rem End local scope for the variables with windows NT shell
if %ERRORLEVEL% equ 0 goto mainEnd

:fail
rem Set variable GRADLE_EXIT_CONSOLE if you need the _script_ return code instead of
rem the _cmd.exe /c_ return code!
set EXIT_CODE=%ERRORLEVEL%
if %EXIT_CODE% equ 0 set EXIT_CODE=1
if not ""=="%GRADLE_EXIT_CONSOLE%" exit %EXIT_CODE%
exit /b %EXIT_CODE%

:mainEnd
if "%OS%"=="Windows_NT" endlocal

:omega
</file>

		Microsoft ResX Schema

		Version 1.3

		The primary goals of this format is to allow a simple XML format 
		that is mostly human readable. The generation and parsing of the 
		various data types are done through the TypeConverter classes 
		associated with the data types.

		Example:

		... ado.net/XML headers & schema ...
		<resheader name="resmimetype">text/microsoft-resx</resheader>
		<resheader name="version">1.3</resheader>
		<resheader name="reader">System.Resources.ResXResourceReader, System.Windows.Forms, ...</resheader>

			[base64 mime encoded serialized .NET Framework object]
		</data>

			[base64 mime encoded string representing a byte array form of the .NET Framework object]
		</data>

		There are any number of "resheader" rows that contain simple 
		name/value pairs.

		Each data row contains a name, and value. The row also contains a 
		type or mimetype. Type corresponds to a .NET class that support 
		text/value conversion through the TypeConverter architecture. 
		Classes that don't support this are serialized and stored with the 
		mimetype set.

		The mimetype is used for serialized objects, and tells the 
		ResXResourceReader how to depersist the object. This is currently not 
		extensible. For a given mimetype the value must be set accordingly:

		Note - application/x-microsoft.net.object.binary.base64 is the format 
		that the ResXResourceWriter will generate, however the reader can

			: and then encoded with base64 encoding.

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/Services/ApiService.cs">
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Models;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.Services
{
    public class ApiService
    {
        const int ClientTimeoutSeconds = 15;
        const string ApiName = "SignalTrack.Api";
        HttpClient client;

        private static readonly Lazy<ApiService> instance =
            new Lazy<ApiService>(() => new ApiService());

        public static ApiService Instance => instance.Value;

        private ApiService()
        {
            ConfigureClient();
        }

        public async Task<bool> CheckConnectionAsync(Uri testUri)
        {
            try
            {
                var responce = await client.GetAsync(testUri);
                var content = await responce.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<PingResponce>();
                return content?.Service == ApiName;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public async Task LogutAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                await client.PostAsJsonAsync("api/auth/logout", new { refreshToken = SessionContext.RefreshToken });
            }
            catch { }
        }

        public async Task<bool> LoginAsync(string loign, string password)
        {
            try
            {
                var request = new LoginRequest
                {
                    Login = loign,
                    Password = password
                };
                var responce = await client.PostAsJsonAsync("api/auth/login", request);
                var content = await responce.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<TokenResponse>();
                var result = responce.IsSuccessStatusCode;
                if (result && content != null)
                {
                    SessionContext.AccessToken = content.AccessToken;
                    SessionContext.RefreshToken = content.RefreshToken;
                    SessionContext.FullName = $"{content.LastName} {content.FirstName} {content.MiddleName}";
                }
                return result;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public async Task<bool> RefreshTokensAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                var responce = await client.PostAsJsonAsync("api/auth/refresh", new { refreshToken = SessionContext.RefreshToken });
                var content = await responce.Content.ReadFromJsonAsync<TokenResponse>();
                var result = responce.IsSuccessStatusCode;
                if (result && content != null)
                {
                    SessionContext.AccessToken = content.AccessToken;
                    SessionContext.RefreshToken = content.RefreshToken;
                }
                return result;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public async Task<UsersResponce?> GetUsersAsync(int page)
        {
            try
            {
                return await client.GetFromJsonAsync<UsersResponce>($"api/auth?page={page}");
            }
            catch
            {
                return null;
            }
        }

        public async Task<UserDto?> GetUserAsync(int id)
        {
            try
            {
                return await client.GetFromJsonAsync<UserDto>($"api/auth/{id}");
            }
            catch
            {
                return null;
            }
        }

        public async Task<bool> DeleteUserAsync(int id)
        {

            }

            }
        }

            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }

        public void ConfigureClient()
        {
            var settings = SettingsService.Load();
            var address = settings.Address;
            var port = settings.Port;
            var scheme = settings.UseHttps ? "https" : "http";

            var rootHandler = new HttpClientHandler();
            var authHandler = new AuthTokenHandler(this)
            {
                InnerHandler = rootHandler
            };

            client = new HttpClient(authHandler)
            {
                BaseAddress = new Uri($"{scheme}://{address}:{port}/"),
                Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(ClientTimeoutSeconds)
            };
        }

        public async Task<EquipmentResponce?> GetEquipmentAsync(int page)

                return await client.GetFromJsonAsync<EquipmentResponce>($"api/equipment/admin-panel?page={page}");
            }

                return null;

        // === Журнал действий администратора (Этап 3) ===
        public async Task<PagedAuditLogsResponse?> GetAuditLogsAsync(
            int page = 1,
            int pageSize = 15,
            string? entityName = null,
            string? action = null,
            string? userLogin = null)
        {
            try
            {
                var query = $"api/audit?page={page}&pageSize={pageSize}";
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entityName))
                    query += $"&entityName={Uri.EscapeDataString(entityName)}";
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(action))
                    query += $"&action={Uri.EscapeDataString(action)}";
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userLogin))
                    query += $"&userLogin={Uri.EscapeDataString(userLogin)}";

                return await client.GetFromJsonAsync<PagedAuditLogsResponse>(query);
            }
            catch
            {
                return null;
            }
        }

        // === Экспорт/импорт конфигурации агента через API (Этап 2) ===
        public async Task<List<DeviceConfig>?> GetAgentConfigAsync()

                return false;

        }

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/Services/AuthTokenHandler.cs">
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Net;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Headers;
using System.Runtime.InteropServices;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.Services
{
    public class AuthTokenHandler(ApiService authService) : DelegatingHandler
    {
        private readonly ApiService apiService = authService;

        protected override async Task<HttpResponseMessage> SendAsync(HttpRequestMessage request, CancellationToken cancellationToken)
        {
            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(SessionContext.AccessToken))
            {
                request.Headers.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", SessionContext.AccessToken);
            }

            var response = await base.SendAsync(request, cancellationToken);

            if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.Unauthorized)
            {
                string requestPath = request.RequestUri?.AbsolutePath ?? string.Empty;

                bool isAuthRequest = requestPath.Contains("/api/auth/login", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                                    requestPath.Contains("/api/auth/refresh", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);

                if (isAuthRequest)
                {
                    request.Headers.Remove("X-Device-Name");
                    request.Headers.Add("X-Device-Name", GetDeviceName());
                }

                if (!isAuthRequest)
                {
                    bool isRefreshed = await TryRefreshTokensAsync();

                    if (isRefreshed)
                    {
                        request.Headers.Authorization = new AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", SessionContext.AccessToken);
                        response.Dispose();
                        return await base.SendAsync(request, cancellationToken);
                    }
                }
            }

            return response;
        }

        private async Task<bool> TryRefreshTokensAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                return await apiService.RefreshTokensAsync();
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        private string GetDeviceName()
        {
            string machineName = Environment.MachineName;

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(machineName) && machineName != "localhost")
            {
                return machineName;
            }

            string osDescription = RuntimeInformation.OSDescription;
            string architecture = RuntimeInformation.ProcessArchitecture.ToString();

            return $"{osDescription} ({architecture})";
        }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/Services/SettingsService.cs">
using Signal.Models;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Json;

namespace Signal.Services
{
    public static class SettingsService
    {
        private static readonly string AppFolderName = "signal";
        private static readonly string FileName = "network_settings.json";

        private static readonly string FolderPath = Path.Combine(
            Environment.GetFolderPath(Environment.SpecialFolder.ApplicationData),
            AppFolderName
        );

        private static readonly string FilePath = Path.Combine(FolderPath, FileName);

        public static void Save(NetworkSettings settings)
        {
            try
            {
                Directory.CreateDirectory(FolderPath);
                string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(settings, new JsonSerializerOptions { WriteIndented = true });
                File.WriteAllText(FilePath, json);
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
            }
        }
        public static NetworkSettings Load()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(FilePath))
                    return new NetworkSettings();

                string json = File.ReadAllText(FilePath);
                return JsonSerializer.Deserialize<NetworkSettings>(json) ?? new NetworkSettings();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                return new NetworkSettings(); 
            }
        }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/AnnotationItemViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using System;
using System.ComponentModel;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class AnnotationItemViewModel : ObservableObject
    {
        private readonly Action onChanged;
        [ObservableProperty]
        string key;
        [ObservableProperty]
        string value;

        public AnnotationItemViewModel(string key, string value, Action onChanged)
        {
            this.key = key;
            this.value = value;
            this.onChanged = onChanged;
        }

        protected override void OnPropertyChanged(PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
        {
            base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
            if (e.PropertyName == nameof(Key) || e.PropertyName == nameof(Value))
                onChanged?.Invoke();
        }
    }
}

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/AuditLogsPageViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class AuditLogsPageViewModel(ApiService apiService) : ViewModelBase
    {
        private const int PageSize = 15;
        private readonly ApiService apiService = apiService;

        public ObservableCollection<AuditLogDto> Logs { get; set; } = new();

        [ObservableProperty]
        private int currentPage = 1;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private int totalPages = 1;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private int totalLogs;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private string? entityFilter;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private string? actionFilter;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private string? userLoginFilter;

        [RelayCommand]
        public async Task LoadAuditLogsAsync()
        {
            var response = await apiService.GetAuditLogsAsync(
                CurrentPage,
                PageSize,
                EntityFilter,
                ActionFilter,
                UserLoginFilter);

            if (response is not null)
            {
                Logs.Clear();
                foreach (var log in response.Logs)
                {
                    Logs.Add(log);
                }

                TotalLogs = response.TotalCount;
                TotalPages = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling((double)TotalLogs / PageSize));
            }

            NextPageCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            PrevPageCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ApplyFilterAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage = 1;
            await LoadAuditLogsAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ResetFilterAsync()
        {
            EntityFilter = string.Empty;
            ActionFilter = string.Empty;
            UserLoginFilter = string.Empty;
            CurrentPage = 1;
            await LoadAuditLogsAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand(CanExecute = nameof(CanGoNextPage))]
        private async Task NextPageAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage++;
            await LoadAuditLogsAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand(CanExecute = nameof(CanGoPrevPage))]
        private async Task PrevPageAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage--;
            await LoadAuditLogsAsync();
        }

        private bool CanGoNextPage() => CurrentPage < TotalPages;
        private bool CanGoPrevPage() => CurrentPage > 1;
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/EquipmentPageViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Models;
using Signal.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class EquipmentPageViewModel(ApiService apiService) : ViewModelBase
    {
        private const int PageSize = 10;
        private readonly ApiService apiService = apiService;

        public ObservableCollection<EquipmentDto> Equipment { get; set; } = new();

        [ObservableProperty]
        int currentPage = 1;
        [ObservableProperty]
        int totalPages = 1;
        [ObservableProperty]
        int totalEquipment;

        [RelayCommand]
        public async Task LoadEquipmentAsync()
        {
            var response = await apiService.GetEquipmentAsync(CurrentPage);
            if (response != null)
            {
                Equipment.Clear();
                foreach (var item in response.Equipment)
                {
                    Equipment.Add(item);
                }
                TotalEquipment = response.TotalEquipment;
                TotalPages = Math.Max(1, (int)Math.Ceiling((double)TotalEquipment / PageSize));
            }
            NextPageCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            PrevPageCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
        }

        [RelayCommand(CanExecute = nameof(CanGoNextPage))]
        async Task NextPageAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage++;
            await LoadEquipmentAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand(CanExecute = nameof(CanGoPrewPage))]
        async Task PrevPageAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage--;
            await LoadEquipmentAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task AddEquipmentAsync()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new EquipmentRedactorPage();
            await LoadEquipmentAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task EditEquipmentAsync(EquipmentDto? item)
        {
            if (item is null)
                return;
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new EquipmentRedactorPage(item);
            await LoadEquipmentAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task DeleteEquipmentAsync(EquipmentDto? item)
        {
            if (item is null) return;
            var result = await apiService.DeleteEquipmentAsync(item.EquipmentId);
            if (result) await LoadEquipmentAsync();
        }

        bool CanGoNextPage() => CurrentPage < TotalPages;
        bool CanGoPrewPage() => CurrentPage > 1;
    }
}

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/EquipmentRedactorPageViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Models;
using Signal.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Net;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Xml.Linq;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class EquipmentRedactorPageViewModel : ViewModelBase
    {
        private readonly ApiService apiService;
        private readonly EquipmentDto? editingEquipment;

        [ObservableProperty]
        string name = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty]
        string serialNumber = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty]
        string model = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty]
        string statusName = "Ok";
        [ObservableProperty]
        string ipAddress = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty]
        bool isEditMode;

        public EquipmentRedactorPageViewModel(ApiService service, EquipmentDto? item = null)
        {
            apiService = service;
            editingEquipment = item;
            IsEditMode = editingEquipment != null;

            if (IsEditMode && editingEquipment != null)
            {
                Name = editingEquipment.Name;
                SerialNumber = editingEquipment.SerialNumber;
                Model = editingEquipment.Model;
                StatusName = editingEquipment.StatusName;
                IpAddress = editingEquipment.IpAddress ?? string.Empty;
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        void Close()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new EquipmentPage();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task SaveAsync()
        {
            if (IsEditMode && editingEquipment != null)
            {
                if (Name != editingEquipment.Name ||
                    SerialNumber != editingEquipment.SerialNumber ||
                    Model != editingEquipment.Model ||
                    StatusName != editingEquipment.StatusName ||
                    IpAddress != (editingEquipment.IpAddress ?? string.Empty))
                {
                    var updateDto = new EquipmentDto(
                        editingEquipment.EquipmentId,
                        SerialNumber,
                        Name,
                        Model,
                        StatusName,
                        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(IpAddress) ? null : IpAddress.Trim(),
                        editingEquipment.IsDeleted,
                        editingEquipment.PollEnabled
                    );

                    await apiService.UpdateEquipmentAsync(editingEquipment.EquipmentId, updateDto);
                }
            }
            else
            {
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Name) && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(SerialNumber))
                {
                    var createDto = new EquipmentDto(
                        0,
                        SerialNumber.Trim(),
                        Name.Trim(),
                        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Model) ? "Other" : Model.Trim(),
                        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(StatusName) ? "Ok" : StatusName.Trim(),
                        string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(IpAddress) ? null : IpAddress.Trim(),
                        false,
                        true
                    );

                    await apiService.AddEquipmentAsync(createDto);
                }
            }

            Close();
        }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/JsonConfigPageViewModel.cs">
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Platform.Storage;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Models;
using Signal.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.Encodings.Web;
using System.Text.Json;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class JsonConfigPageViewModel : ViewModelBase
    {
        readonly TopLevel? topLevel;
        readonly string localConfigPath = Path.Combine("configs", "devices.json");
        readonly JsonSerializerOptions jsonOptions = new()
        {
            WriteIndented = true,
            PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true,
            Encoder = JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping
        };


        public ObservableCollection<DeviceConfig> Devices { get; set; } = new();
        public ObservableCollection<AnnotationItemViewModel> CurrentAnnotations { get; set; } = new();

        [ObservableProperty]
        DeviceConfig? selectedDevice;
        [ObservableProperty]
        ParamConfig? selectedParam;
        [ObservableProperty]
        AnnotationItemViewModel? selectedAnnotation;
        [ObservableProperty]
        string statusMessage = string.Empty;

        public JsonConfigPageViewModel(TopLevel? topLevel)
        {
            this.topLevel = topLevel;
            LoadConfig();
        }

        partial void OnSelectedDeviceChanged(DeviceConfig? value)
        {
            SelectedParam = null;
            CurrentAnnotations.Clear();
        }

        partial void OnSelectedParamChanged(ParamConfig? value)
        {
            CurrentAnnotations.Clear();
            if (value?.Annotations is null) return;

            foreach (var kvp in value.Annotations)
            {
                CurrentAnnotations.Add(new AnnotationItemViewModel(kvp.Key, kvp.Value, SyncAnnotationsToDictionary));
            }
        }

        private void SyncAnnotationsToDictionary()
        {
            if (SelectedParam is null) return;

            SelectedParam.Annotations ??= new Dictionary<string, string>();
            SelectedParam.Annotations.Clear();

            foreach (var item in CurrentAnnotations)
            {
                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(item.Key))
                {
                    SelectedParam.Annotations[item.Key] = item.Value ?? string.Empty;
                }
            }

            if (SelectedParam.Annotations.Count == 0) SelectedParam.Annotations = null;
        }

        private void LoadConfig()
        {
            try
            {
                if (!File.Exists(localConfigPath))
                {
                    var dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(localConfigPath);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir)) Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                    File.WriteAllText(localConfigPath, "[]");
                }

                string json = File.ReadAllText(localConfigPath);
                var list = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<DeviceConfig>>(json, jsonOptions) ?? new List<DeviceConfig>();
                Devices.Clear();
                foreach (var item in list)
                {
                    Devices.Add(item);
                }
                StatusMessage = "Конфигурация успешно загружена";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = $"Ошибка чтения файла: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void SaveConfig()
        {
            try
            {
                SyncAnnotationsToDictionary();

                var dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(localConfigPath);
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir)) Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);

                string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(Devices.ToList(), jsonOptions);
                File.WriteAllText(localConfigPath, json);
                StatusMessage = "Изменения сохранены";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = $"Ошибка сохранения: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ExportConfigAsync()
        {
            if (topLevel is null)
                return;

            SyncAnnotationsToDictionary();

            var options = new FilePickerSaveOptions
            {
                Title = "Экспорт JSON-конфигурации",
                DefaultExtension = "json",
                SuggestedFileName = "devices.json",
                FileTypeChoices = new[]
                {
                new FilePickerFileType("JSON файлы (*.json)") { Patterns = new[] { "*.json" } },
                new FilePickerFileType("Все файлы (*.*)") { Patterns = new[] { "*.*" } }
            }
            };

            var file = await topLevel.StorageProvider.SaveFilePickerAsync(options);
            if (file is not null)
            {
                try
                {
                    string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(Devices.ToList(), jsonOptions);
                    await using var stream = await file.OpenWriteAsync();
                    await using var writer = new StreamWriter(stream);
                    await writer.WriteAsync(json);
                    StatusMessage = "Файл успешно экспортирован";
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    StatusMessage = $"Ошибка экспорта: {ex.Message}";
                }
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ImportConfigAsync()
        {
            if (topLevel is null)
                return;

            var options = new FilePickerOpenOptions
            {
                Title = "Импорт JSON-конфигурации",
                AllowMultiple = false,
                FileTypeFilter = new[]
                {
                    new FilePickerFileType("JSON файлы (*.json)") { Patterns = new[] { "*.json" } },
                    new FilePickerFileType("Все файлы (*.*)") { Patterns = new[] { "*.*" } }
                }
            };

            var files = await topLevel.StorageProvider.OpenFilePickerAsync(options);
            var file = files.FirstOrDefault();
            if (file is not null)
            {
                try
                {
                    await using var stream = await file.OpenReadAsync();
                    using var reader = new StreamReader(stream);
                    string json = await reader.ReadToEndAsync();

                    var list = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<DeviceConfig>>(json, jsonOptions);
                    if (list is null)
                    {
                        StatusMessage = "Ошибка: неверный формат JSON-файла";
                        return;
                    }

                    SelectedDevice = null;
                    SelectedParam = null;
                    CurrentAnnotations.Clear();
                    Devices.Clear();

                    foreach (var item in list)
                    {
                        Devices.Add(item);
                    }

                    var dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(localConfigPath);
                    if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir)) Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                    string normalizedJson = JsonSerializer.Serialize(Devices.ToList(), jsonOptions);
                    File.WriteAllText(localConfigPath, normalizedJson);

                    SelectedDevice = Devices.FirstOrDefault();
                    StatusMessage = $"Файл успешно импортирован (устройств: {Devices.Count})";
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    StatusMessage = $"Ошибка импорта файла: {ex.Message}";
                }
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task UploadToServerAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                SyncAnnotationsToDictionary();
                SaveConfig();

                StatusMessage = "Загрузка конфигурации на сервер...";
                bool isSaved = await ApiService.Instance.SaveAgentConfigAsync(Devices.ToList());
                StatusMessage = isSaved
                    ? "Конфигурация успешно загружена на сервер"
                    : "Ошибка загрузки конфигурации на сервер";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = $"Ошибка загрузки на сервер: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task ImportFromServerAsync()
        {
            try
            {
                SyncAnnotationsToDictionary();
                StatusMessage = "Импорт конфигурации с сервера...";

                var serverDevices = await ApiService.Instance.GetAgentConfigAsync();
                if (serverDevices is null)
                {
                    StatusMessage = "Ошибка импорта конфигурации с сервера";
                    return;
                }

                var existingByIp = Devices
                    .Where(d => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(d.Ip))
                    .GroupBy(d => d.Ip.Trim())
                    .ToDictionary(g => g.Key, g => g.First());

                SelectedDevice = null;
                SelectedParam = null;
                CurrentAnnotations.Clear();
                Devices.Clear();

                foreach (var serverDev in serverDevices)
                {
                    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(serverDev.Ip) &&
                        existingByIp.TryGetValue(serverDev.Ip.Trim(), out var localDev))
                    {
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(serverDev.Login))
                            serverDev.Login = localDev.Login;
                        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(serverDev.Password))
                            serverDev.Password = localDev.Password;

                        if (localDev.Params is not null && serverDev.Params is not null)
                        {
                            foreach (var sParam in serverDev.Params)
                            {
                                var matchedLocalParam = localDev.Params.FirstOrDefault(
                                    lp => lp.Command == sParam.Command || lp.Name == sParam.Name);
                                if (matchedLocalParam?.Annotations is not null && sParam.Annotations is null)
                                {
                                    sParam.Annotations = new Dictionary<string, string>(matchedLocalParam.Annotations);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Devices.Add(serverDev);
                }

                var dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(localConfigPath);
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir)) Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
                string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(Devices.ToList(), jsonOptions);
                File.WriteAllText(localConfigPath, json);

                SelectedDevice = Devices.FirstOrDefault();
                StatusMessage = $"Импортировано с сервера устройств: {Devices.Count}";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                StatusMessage = $"Ошибка импорта с сервера: {ex.Message}";
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void AddParam()
        {
            if (SelectedDevice is null)
                return;

            var newParam = new ParamConfig { Command = "cmd?", Name = "new_param" };
            var updatedList = SelectedDevice.Params.ToList();
            updatedList.Add(newParam);
            SelectedDevice.Params = updatedList;

            var currentDevice = SelectedDevice;
            SelectedDevice = null;
            SelectedDevice = currentDevice;
            SelectedParam = newParam;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void DeleteParam()
        {
            if (SelectedDevice is null || SelectedParam is null)
                return;

            var updatedList = SelectedDevice.Params.ToList();
            updatedList.Remove(SelectedParam);
            SelectedDevice.Params = updatedList;

            var currentDevice = SelectedDevice;
            SelectedDevice = null;
            SelectedDevice = currentDevice;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void AddAnnotation()
        {
            if (SelectedParam is null) return;

            var newItem = new AnnotationItemViewModel("0", "Описание", SyncAnnotationsToDictionary);
            CurrentAnnotations.Add(newItem);
            SyncAnnotationsToDictionary();
            SelectedAnnotation = newItem;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void DeleteAnnotation()
        {
            if (SelectedAnnotation is null) return;

            CurrentAnnotations.Remove(SelectedAnnotation);
            SyncAnnotationsToDictionary();
            SelectedAnnotation = null;
        }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/LoginWindowViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Services;
using Signal.Views;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using System.Windows.Input;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class LoginWindowViewModel(LoginWindow window) : ViewModelBase
    {
        [ObservableProperty]
        private string login = string.Empty;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private string password = string.Empty;

        [ObservableProperty]
        private string errorMessage = string.Empty;

        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenSettings()
        {
            var settingsWindow = new SettingsWindow();
            settingsWindow.ShowDialog(window);
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task LoginAsync()
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Login) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Password))
            {
                ErrorMessage = "Заполните все поля";
                return;
            }

            ErrorMessage = string.Empty;

            try
            {
                if (await ApiService.Instance.LoginAsync(Login, Password))
                {
                    var mainWindow = new MainWindow();
                    mainWindow.Show();
                    window.Close();
                    return;
                }
                ErrorMessage = "Неверный логин или пароль";
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                ErrorMessage = $"Ошибка подключения \n{ex.Message}";
            }
        }

    }
}

        {
            NavigateToUsersPage();
            currentWindow = window;
            UserFullName = SessionContext.FullName ?? "-";
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void ToggleMenu() => IsMenuOpen = !IsMenuOpen;

        [RelayCommand]
        private void NavigateToUsersPage()
        {
            var pageType = new UsersPage().GetType();
            if (pageType != null)
                CurrentPage = Activator.CreateInstance(pageType);
            IsMenuOpen = false;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private async Task OpenSettingsAsync()
        {
            IsMenuOpen = false;

            var settingsWindow = new SettingsWindow();

            await settingsWindow.ShowDialog(currentWindow);
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenUsersPage()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new UsersPage();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenEquipmentPage()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new EquipmentPage();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenReadingTypePage()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new ReadingTypesPage();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenJsonConfigPage()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new JsonConfigPage();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void OpenAuditLogsPage()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new AuditLogsPage();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        private void ExitApplication()
        {
            if (Application.Current?.ApplicationLifetime is IClassicDesktopStyleApplicationLifetime desktop)
                desktop.Shutdown();
        }

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/ReadingTypeRedactorPageViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Models;
using Signal.Services;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class ReadingTypeRedactorPageViewModel : ViewModelBase
    {
        private readonly ApiService apiService;
        private readonly ReadingTypeDto editingReadingType;

        [ObservableProperty] 
        string name = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        string identifier = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        string? unit;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        decimal? minValue;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        decimal? maxValue;

        public ReadingTypeRedactorPageViewModel(ApiService service, ReadingTypeDto item)
        {
            apiService = service;
            editingReadingType = item;

            Name = editingReadingType.Name;
            Identifier = editingReadingType.Identifier;
            Unit = editingReadingType.Unit;
            MinValue = editingReadingType.MinValue;
            MaxValue = editingReadingType.MaxValue;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        void Close()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new ReadingTypesPage();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task SaveAsync()
        {
            if (Name != editingReadingType.Name ||
                Identifier != editingReadingType.Identifier ||
                Unit != editingReadingType.Unit ||
                MinValue != editingReadingType.MinValue ||
                MaxValue != editingReadingType.MaxValue)
            {
                var updateDto = new ReadingTypeDto
                {
                    ReadingTypeId = editingReadingType.ReadingTypeId,
                    Name = Name,
                    Identifier = Identifier,
                    Unit = Unit,
                    MinValue = MinValue,
                    MaxValue = MaxValue
                };

                await apiService.UpdateReadingTypeAsync(editingReadingType.ReadingTypeId, updateDto);
            }

            Close();
        }
    }
}
