<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/ReadingTypesPageViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Models;
using Signal.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class ReadingTypesPageViewModel(ApiService apiService) : ViewModelBase
    {
        private const int PageSize = 10;
        private readonly ApiService apiService = apiService;

        public ObservableCollection<ReadingTypeDto> ReadingTypes { get; set; } = new();

        [ObservableProperty] 
        int currentPage = 1;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        int totalPages = 1;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        int totalReadingTypes;

        [RelayCommand]
        public async Task LoadReadingTypesAsync()
        {
            var response = await apiService.GetReadingTypesAsync(CurrentPage);
            if (response != null)
            {
                ReadingTypes.Clear();
                foreach (var item in response.ReadingTypes)
                {
                    ReadingTypes.Add(item);
                }
                TotalReadingTypes = response.TotalReadingTypes;
                TotalPages = (int)Math.Ceiling((double)TotalReadingTypes / PageSize);
            }
            NextPageCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            PrevPageCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
        }

        [RelayCommand(CanExecute = nameof(CanGoNextPage))]
        async Task NextPageAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage++;
            await LoadReadingTypesAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand(CanExecute = nameof(CanGoPrewPage))]
        async Task PrevPageAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage--;
            await LoadReadingTypesAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task EditReadingTypeAsync(ReadingTypeDto? item)
        {
            if (item is null) 
                return;
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new ReadingTypeRedactorPage(item);
            await LoadReadingTypesAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task DeleteReadingTypeAsync(ReadingTypeDto? item)
        {
            if (item is null) return;
            var result = await apiService.DeleteReadingTypeAsync(item.ReadingTypeId);
            if (result) await LoadReadingTypesAsync();
        }

        bool CanGoNextPage() => CurrentPage < TotalPages;
        bool CanGoPrewPage() => CurrentPage > 1;
    }
}

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/SettingsWindowViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Models;
using Signal.Services;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class SettingsWindowViewModel : ViewModelBase
    {
        [ObservableProperty]
        string address = "";
        [ObservableProperty]
        string port = "";
        [ObservableProperty]
        bool useHttps = true;
        [ObservableProperty]
        [NotifyCanExecuteChangedFor(nameof(SaveSettingsCommand))]
        bool isConnected;
        [ObservableProperty]
        bool isSaved;
        [ObservableProperty]
        [NotifyCanExecuteChangedFor(nameof(TryConnectCommand))]
        bool isDataCorrect;
        public ObservableCollection<string> Errors { get; } = new();
        [ObservableProperty]
        string connectionState = "Проверить";

        string savedAddress;
        string savedPort;
        bool savedUseHttps;

        public SettingsWindowViewModel()
        {
            var settings = SettingsService.Load();
            savedAddress = settings.Address;
            savedPort = settings.Port;
            savedUseHttps = settings.UseHttps;
            Address = savedAddress;
            Port = savedPort;
            UseHttps = savedUseHttps;
            IsConnected = IsSaved = Address == savedAddress && Port == savedPort && UseHttps == savedUseHttps;
        }

        void ValidateInput()
        {
            Errors.Clear();
            IsDataCorrect = true;
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Address))
            {
                Errors.Add("Адрес не может быть пустым");
                IsDataCorrect = false;
            }

            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Port))
            {
                Errors.Add("Порт не может быть пустым");
                IsDataCorrect = false;
            }
            else if (!Int32.TryParse(Port, out int intPort) || intPort < 0 || intPort > 65535)
            {
                Errors.Add("Порт может быть только числом от 0 до 65535");
                IsDataCorrect = false;
            }

            IsSaved = IsConnected = Port == savedPort && Address == savedAddress && UseHttps == savedUseHttps;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task TryConnectAsync()
        {
            ConnectionState = "Подключение...";
            var scheme = UseHttps ? "https" : "http";
            IsConnected = await ApiService.Instance.CheckConnectionAsync(new Uri($"{scheme}://{Address}:{Port}/api/ping"));
            ConnectionState = "Проверить";
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        void SaveSettings()
        {
            var settings = new NetworkSettings()
            {
                Address = Address,
                Port = Port,
                UseHttps = UseHttps
            };
            SettingsService.Save(settings);
            ApiService.Instance.ConfigureClient();

            var loaded = SettingsService.Load();
            savedAddress = loaded.Address;
            savedPort = loaded.Port;
            savedUseHttps = loaded.UseHttps;

            IsSaved = Address == savedAddress && Port == savedPort && UseHttps == savedUseHttps;
        }

        partial void OnAddressChanged(string value) => ValidateInput();
        partial void OnPortChanged(string value) => ValidateInput();
        partial void OnUseHttpsChanged(bool value) => ValidateInput();
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewModels/UserRedactorPageViewModel.cs">
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Models;
using Signal.Services;
using Signal.Views;
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.ObjectModel;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class UserRedactorPageViewModel : ViewModelBase
    {
        private readonly ApiService apiService;
        private readonly UserDto? editingUser;

        public ObservableCollection<RoleSelectionItem> Roles { get; } = new();

        [ObservableProperty] 
        string login = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        string secondName = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        string firstName = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty]
        string lastName = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty]
        string password = string.Empty;
        [ObservableProperty] 
        bool isEditMode;

        public UserRedactorPageViewModel(ApiService service, UserDto? user = null)
        {
            apiService = service;
            editingUser = user;
            IsEditMode = editingUser != null;
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task InitializeAsync()
        {
            var allRoles = await apiService.GetRolesAsync() ?? new List<string>();

            if (IsEditMode && editingUser != null)
            {
                Login = editingUser.Login;
                SecondName = editingUser.SecondName;
                FirstName = editingUser.FirstName;
                LastName = editingUser.LastName ?? string.Empty;

                foreach (var role in allRoles)
                {
                    Roles.Add(new RoleSelectionItem
                    {
                        RoleName = role,
                        IsSelected = editingUser.RoleNames.Contains(role)
                    });
                }
            }
            else
            {
                foreach (var role in allRoles)
                {
                    Roles.Add(new RoleSelectionItem { RoleName = role, IsSelected = false });
                }
            }
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        void Close()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new UsersPage();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task SaveAsync()
        {
            if (IsEditMode && editingUser != null)
            {
                if (Login != editingUser.Login || SecondName != editingUser.SecondName ||
                    FirstName != editingUser.FirstName || LastName != editingUser.LastName)
                {
                    var updateDto = new UserDto
                    {
                        UserId = editingUser.UserId,
                        Login = Login,
                        SecondName = SecondName,
                        FirstName = FirstName,
                        LastName = LastName,
                        RoleNames = new List<string>()
                    };
                    await apiService.UpdateUserAsync(editingUser.UserId, updateDto);
                }

                var currentRoles = Roles.Where(r => r.IsSelected).Select(r => r.RoleName).ToList();
                var oldRoles = editingUser.RoleNames;
                if (!currentRoles.SequenceEqual(oldRoles))
                {
                    var rolesDto = new UpdateUserRolesDto { Roles = currentRoles };
                    await apiService.UpdateUserRolesAsync(editingUser.UserId, rolesDto);
                }

                if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(Password))
                    await apiService.ResetUserPasswordAsync(editingUser.UserId, Password);
            }
            else
            {
                var registerDto = new RegisterRequest
                {
                    Login = Login,
                    HashedPassword = Password, 
                    FirstName = FirstName,
                    SecondName = SecondName, 
                    LastName = LastName,  
                    Role = Roles.FirstOrDefault(r => r.IsSelected)?.RoleName 
                };
                await apiService.AddUserAsync(registerDto);
            }

            Close();
        }
    }
}
</file>

using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.ComponentModel;
using CommunityToolkit.Mvvm.Input;
using Signal.Dtos;
using Signal.Models;
using Signal.Services;

using System.Collections.ObjectModel;

namespace Signal.ViewModels
{
    public partial class UsersPageViewModel(ApiService apiService) : ViewModelBase
    {
        const int PageSize = 10;

        private readonly ApiService apiService = apiService;

        public ObservableCollection<UserDto> Users { get; set; } = new();

        [ObservableProperty]
        int currentPage = 1;
        [ObservableProperty]
        int totalPages = 1;
        [ObservableProperty]
        int totalUsers;

        [RelayCommand]
        public async Task LoadUsersAsync()
        {
            var response = await apiService.GetUsersAsync(CurrentPage);

            if (response is not null)
            {
                Users.Clear();
                foreach (var user in response.Users)
                    Users.Add(user);
                TotalUsers = response.TotalUsers;
                TotalPages = (int)Math.Ceiling((double)TotalUsers / PageSize);
            }
            NextPageCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
            PrevPageCommand.NotifyCanExecuteChanged();
        }

        [RelayCommand(CanExecute = nameof(CanGoNextPage))]
        async Task NextPageAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage++;
            await LoadUsersAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand(CanExecute = nameof(CanGoPrewPage))]
        async Task PrevPageAsync()
        {
            CurrentPage--;
            await LoadUsersAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task EditUserAsync(UserDto? user)
        {
            if (user is null) 
                return;
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new UserRedactorPage(user);
            await LoadUsersAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task DeleteUserAsync(UserDto? user)
        {
            if (user is null) return;
            var result = await apiService.DeleteUserAsync(user.UserId);
            if (result) await LoadUsersAsync();
        }

        [RelayCommand]
        async Task AddUserAsync()
        {
            MainViewModelHandler.Instance.CurrentPage = new UserRedactorPage();
            await LoadUsersAsync();
        }

        bool CanGoNextPage()
            => CurrentPage < TotalPages;

        bool CanGoPrewPage()
            => CurrentPage > 1;
    }
}

        public override void Initialize()
        {
            AvaloniaXamlLoader.Load(this);
        }

        public override void OnFrameworkInitializationCompleted()
        {
            var services = new ServiceCollection();
            ConfigureServices(services);


            if (ApplicationLifetime is IClassicDesktopStyleApplicationLifetime desktop)
            {
                desktop.MainWindow = new LoginWindow();

                desktop.Exit += OnApplicationExit;
            }

            base.OnFrameworkInitializationCompleted();
        }

        private void ConfigureServices(IServiceCollection services)
        {

        }

        private async void OnApplicationExit(object? sender, ControlledApplicationLifetimeExitEventArgs e)
        {
            try
            {
                if (SessionContext.AccessToken is not null)
                    await ApiService.Instance.LogutAsync();
            }
            catch { }
        }
    }

<file path="Signal.desktop/Signal/ViewLocator.cs">
using Avalonia.Controls;
using Avalonia.Controls.Templates;
using Signal.ViewModels;
using System;
using System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;

namespace Signal
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Given a view model, returns the corresponding view if possible.
    /// </summary>
    [RequiresUnreferencedCode(
        "Default implementation of ViewLocator involves reflection which may be trimmed away.",
        Url = "https://docs.avaloniaui.net/docs/concepts/view-locator")]
    public class ViewLocator : IDataTemplate
    {
        public Control? Build(object? param)
        {
            if (param is null)
                return null;

            var name = param.GetType().FullName!.Replace("ViewModel", "View", StringComparison.Ordinal);
            var type = Type.GetType(name);

            if (type != null)
            {
                return (Control)Activator.CreateInstance(type)!;
            }

            return new TextBlock { Text = "Not Found: " + name };
        }

        public bool Match(object? data)
        {
            return data is ViewModelBase;
        }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.Server/Signal.agent/Program.cs">
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Hosting;
using Signal.agent.Models;
using Signal.Server.Data.Dtos;
using System.Net.Http.Json;
using System.Net.Sockets;
using System.Text;
using System.Text.Encodings.Web;
using System.Text.Json;

class Program
{
    static async Task Main(string[] args)
    {
        Console.Write("Введите IP-адрес или домен API сервера: ");
        string apiHost = Console.ReadLine()?.Trim() ?? "localhost";
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(apiHost)) apiHost = "localhost";

        Console.Write("Введите порт API сервера: ");
        string apiPort = Console.ReadLine()?.Trim() ?? "5000";
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(apiPort)) apiPort = "5000";

        string baseApiUrl = $"http://{apiHost}:{apiPort}/";

        var httpClient = new HttpClient
        {
            BaseAddress = new Uri(baseApiUrl),
            Timeout = TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10)
        };

        var configManager = new AgentConfigurationManager(httpClient);
        var offlineQueue = new OfflineTelemetryQueue("telemetry_queue.jsonl");

        // Поддержка CLI-аргументов или интерактивного выбора режима синхронизации конфигурации
        if (args.Contains("--pull-config"))
        {
            await configManager.PullConfigFromServerAsync("devices.json");
        }
        else if (args.Contains("--push-config"))
        {
            await configManager.PushConfigToServerAsync("devices.json");
        }
        else if (args.Length >= 2 && args[0] == "--import-file")
        {
            await configManager.ImportExternalConfigAsync(args[1], "devices.json");
        }
        else if (args.Length >= 2 && args[0] == "--export-file")
        {
            await configManager.ExportConfigToFileAsync("devices.json", args[1]);
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Выберите действие при запуске: [Enter] Обычный запуск | [1] Загрузить конфиг с сервера | [2] Выгрузить локальный конфиг на сервер | [3] Экспорт в файл | [4] Импорт из файла");
            string? choice = Console.ReadLine()?.Trim();
            if (choice == "1")
                await configManager.PullConfigFromServerAsync("devices.json");
            else if (choice == "2")
                await configManager.PushConfigToServerAsync("devices.json");
            else if (choice == "3")
            {
                Console.Write("Укажите путь для экспорта JSON: ");
                var target = Console.ReadLine()?.Trim();
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(target))
                    await configManager.ExportConfigToFileAsync("devices.json", target);
            }
            else if (choice == "4")
            {
                Console.Write("Укажите путь к импортируемому JSON: ");
                var source = Console.ReadLine()?.Trim();
                if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(source))
                    await configManager.ImportExternalConfigAsync(source, "devices.json");
            }
        }

        IHostBuilder hostBuilder = Host.CreateDefaultBuilder(args)
            .ConfigureServices((hostContext, services) =>
            {
                services.AddSingleton(httpClient);
                services.AddSingleton(configManager);
                services.AddSingleton(offlineQueue);
                services.AddHostedService<NetworkPollingService>();
            });

        await hostBuilder.RunConsoleAsync();
    }
}

public class AgentConfigurationManager(HttpClient httpClient)
{
    private readonly HttpClient _httpClient = httpClient;
    private readonly JsonSerializerOptions _jsonOptions = new()
    {
        PropertyNameCaseInsensitive = true,
        WriteIndented = true,
        Encoder = JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping
    };

    public async Task<List<DeviceConfig>> LoadLocalConfigAsync(string filePath = "devices.json")
    {
        try
        {
            if (!File.Exists(filePath))
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[КОНФИГ] Локальный файл {filePath} не найден. Пробуем получить с сервера...");
                var pulled = await PullConfigFromServerAsync(filePath);
                return pulled;
            }

            string jsonString = await File.ReadAllTextAsync(filePath);
            var devices = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<DeviceConfig>>(jsonString, _jsonOptions);
            return devices ?? new List<DeviceConfig>();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Ошибка чтения конфигурации {filePath}: {ex.Message}");
            return new List<DeviceConfig>();
        }
    }

    public async Task<List<DeviceConfig>> PullConfigFromServerAsync(string targetFilePath = "devices.json")
    {
        try
        {
            var serverDevices = await _httpClient.GetFromJsonAsync<List<DeviceConfig>>("api/equipment/agent-config", _jsonOptions);
            if (serverDevices != null && serverDevices.Count > 0)
            {
                // Сохраняем локальные логины/пароли Telnet, если они уже были в локальном файле
                if (File.Exists(targetFilePath))
                {
                    var existing = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<DeviceConfig>>(await File.ReadAllTextAsync(targetFilePath), _jsonOptions) ?? new();
                    foreach (var dev in serverDevices)
                    {
                        var match = existing.FirstOrDefault(x => x.Ip == dev.Ip);
                        if (match != null)
                        {
                            dev.Login ??= match.Login;
                            dev.Password ??= match.Password;
                        }
                    }
                }

                string json = JsonSerializer.Serialize(serverDevices, _jsonOptions);
                await File.WriteAllTextAsync(targetFilePath, json);
                Console.WriteLine($"[КОНФИГ] Конфигурация ({serverDevices.Count} устр.) успешно загружена с сервера в {targetFilePath}");
                return serverDevices;
            }
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Не удалось получить конфигурацию с сервера: {ex.Message}");
        }
        return new List<DeviceConfig>();
    }

    public async Task<bool> PushConfigToServerAsync(string sourceFilePath = "devices.json")
    {
        try
        {
            if (!File.Exists(sourceFilePath))
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Файл {sourceFilePath} не существует для выгрузки.");
                return false;
            }

            var devices = await LoadLocalConfigAsync(sourceFilePath);
            var response = await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync("api/equipment/agent-config", devices, _jsonOptions);
            if (response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[КОНФИГ] Локальная конфигурация ({devices.Count} устр.) успешно выгружена в БД сервера (equipment_command).");
                return true;
            }
            Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Сервер вернул статус {response.StatusCode} при выгрузке конфигурации.");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Не удалось выгрузить конфигурацию на сервер: {ex.Message}");
        }
        return false;
    }

    public async Task ExportConfigToFileAsync(string sourceFilePath, string exportFilePath)
    {
        var devices = await LoadLocalConfigAsync(sourceFilePath);
        var dir = Path.GetDirectoryName(exportFilePath);
        if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(dir)) Directory.CreateDirectory(dir);
        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(exportFilePath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(devices, _jsonOptions));
        Console.WriteLine($"[КОНФИГ] Конфигурация экспортирована в файл: {exportFilePath}");
    }

    public async Task ImportExternalConfigAsync(string importFilePath, string targetFilePath = "devices.json")
    {
        if (!File.Exists(importFilePath))
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Импортируемый файл не найден: {importFilePath}");
            return;
        }
        string raw = await File.ReadAllTextAsync(importFilePath);
        var devices = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<List<DeviceConfig>>(raw, _jsonOptions) ?? new List<DeviceConfig>();
        await File.WriteAllTextAsync(targetFilePath, JsonSerializer.Serialize(devices, _jsonOptions));
        Console.WriteLine($"[КОНФИГ] Импортировано устройств: {devices.Count} в {targetFilePath}");
        await PushConfigToServerAsync(targetFilePath);
    }
}

public class OfflineTelemetryQueue(string queueFilePath = "telemetry_queue.jsonl")
{
    private readonly string _queueFilePath = queueFilePath;
    private readonly SemaphoreSlim _fileLock = new(1, 1);

    public async Task EnqueueAsync(CreateReadingLogDto dto)
    {
        dto.WriteDateTime ??= DateTime.UtcNow;
        await _fileLock.WaitAsync();
        try
        {
            string line = JsonSerializer.Serialize(dto);
            await File.AppendAllTextAsync(_queueFilePath, line + Environment.NewLine, Encoding.UTF8);
            Console.WriteLine($"[ОФЛАЙН-КЭШ] Метрика сохранена локально: IP={dto.IpAddress}, Param={dto.ReadingTypeIdentifier}, Val={dto.Value}, Time={dto.WriteDateTime:O}");
        }
        finally
        {
            _fileLock.Release();
        }
    }

    public async Task FlushIfAvailableAsync(HttpClient httpClient, int batchSize = 50)
    {
        if (!File.Exists(_queueFilePath)) return;

        await _fileLock.WaitAsync();
        try
        {
            var lines = (await File.ReadAllLinesAsync(_queueFilePath, Encoding.UTF8))
                .Where(l => !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(l))
                .ToList();

            if (lines.Count == 0)
            {
                File.Delete(_queueFilePath);
                return;
            }

            var allCached = new List<CreateReadingLogDto>();
            foreach (var line in lines)
            {
                try
                {
                    var item = JsonSerializer.Deserialize<CreateReadingLogDto>(line);
                    if (item != null) allCached.Add(item);
                }
                catch { }
            }

            // Сортируем строго по хронологии снятия показаний
            allCached = allCached.OrderBy(x => x.WriteDateTime ?? DateTime.MinValue).ToList();

            int sentCount = 0;
            while (sentCount < allCached.Count)
            {
                var batch = allCached.Skip(sentCount).Take(batchSize).ToList();
                var response = await httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync("api/equipment/logs/batch", batch);
                if (!response.IsSuccessStatusCode)
                {
                    // Сохраняем неотправленный остаток обратно на диск
                    var remaining = allCached.Skip(sentCount).Select(x => JsonSerializer.Serialize(x));
                    await File.WriteAllLinesAsync(_queueFilePath, remaining, Encoding.UTF8);
                    return;
                }
                sentCount += batch.Count;
            }

            File.Delete(_queueFilePath);
            Console.WriteLine($"[СИНХРОНИЗАЦИЯ] Успешно дослано {sentCount} закэшированных записей телеметрии на сервер.");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[ОФЛАЙН-КЭШ] Сервер пока недоступен для досылки кэша: {ex.Message}");
        }
        finally
        {
            _fileLock.Release();
        }
    }
}

public class NetworkPollingService(
    HttpClient httpClient,
    AgentConfigurationManager configManager,
    OfflineTelemetryQueue offlineQueue) : BackgroundService
{
    private readonly HttpClient _httpClient = httpClient;
    private readonly AgentConfigurationManager _configManager = configManager;
    private readonly OfflineTelemetryQueue _offlineQueue = offlineQueue;

    protected override async Task ExecuteAsync(CancellationToken stoppingToken)
    {
        while (!stoppingToken.IsCancellationRequested)
        {
            // Перед новым циклом опроса пробуем дослать накопленный офлайн-кэш
            await _offlineQueue.FlushIfAvailableAsync(_httpClient);

            List<DeviceConfig> devices = await _configManager.LoadLocalConfigAsync("devices.json");

            foreach (var device in devices)
            {
                if (stoppingToken.IsCancellationRequested) break;
                await PollDeviceAsync(device);
            }

            await Task.Delay(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(10), stoppingToken);
        }
    }

    private async Task PollDeviceAsync(DeviceConfig device)
    {
        using var tcpClient = new TcpClient();
        try
        {
            tcpClient.ReceiveTimeout = 5000;
            tcpClient.SendTimeout = 5000;

            var connectTask = tcpClient.ConnectAsync(device.Ip, 23);
            if (await Task.WhenAny(connectTask, Task.Delay(5000)) != connectTask)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Таймаут подключения к IP {device.Ip}");
                return;
            }

            using var stream = tcpClient.GetStream();
            using var reader = new StreamReader(stream, Encoding.ASCII);
            using var writer = new StreamWriter(stream, Encoding.ASCII) { AutoFlush = true };

            string prompt = "(Q=Quit) Telnet-->";

            await ReadUntilTimeoutOrTokenAsync(reader, "Enter name:");
            await writer.WriteLineAsync(device.Login);

            await ReadUntilTimeoutOrTokenAsync(reader, "Enter password:");
            await writer.WriteLineAsync(device.Password);

            string response = await ReadUntilTimeoutOrTokenAsync(reader, prompt);
            if (!response.Contains(prompt))
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Не удалось войти в CLI на {device.Ip}. Неверный логин/пароль");
                return;
            }

            foreach (var param in device.Params)
            {
                await writer.WriteLineAsync(param.Command);

                string rawResult = await ReadUntilTimeoutOrTokenAsync(reader, prompt);
                string cleanedResult = ExtractValueFromResponse(rawResult);
                string finalValue = ProcessValue(cleanedResult, param);

                Console.WriteLine($"IP: {device.Ip} | Команда: {param.Command} | Итог: {finalValue}");

                var logDto = new CreateReadingLogDto
                {
                    IpAddress = device.Ip,
                    ReadingTypeIdentifier = param.Name,
                    Value = finalValue,
                    WriteDateTime = DateTime.UtcNow
                };

                try
                {
                    var apiResponse = await _httpClient.PostAsJsonAsync("api/equipment/logs", logDto);
                    if (!apiResponse.IsSuccessStatusCode)
                    {
                        await _offlineQueue.EnqueueAsync(logDto);
                    }
                    else
                    {
                        // Если связь есть, попутно досылаем старые пакеты из очереди
                        await _offlineQueue.FlushIfAvailableAsync(_httpClient);
                    }
                }
                catch (Exception ex)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine($"[СЕТЬ НЕДОСТУПНА] IP: {device.Ip} | Перевод пакета {param.Name}={finalValue} в локальную очередь ({ex.Message})");
                    await _offlineQueue.EnqueueAsync(logDto);
                }
            }

            await writer.WriteLineAsync("Q");
            Console.WriteLine("------");
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"[ОШИБКА] Исключение при опросе {device.Ip}: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    private async Task<string> ReadUntilTimeoutOrTokenAsync(StreamReader reader, string token)
    {
        var sb = new StringBuilder();
        char[] buffer = new char[1];
        using var cts = new CancellationTokenSource(TimeSpan.FromSeconds(5));

        try
        {
            while (!cts.Token.IsCancellationRequested)
            {
                int readCount = await reader.ReadAsync(buffer, 0, 1);
                if (readCount == 0) break;
                sb.Append(buffer[0]);
                if (sb.ToString().Contains(token)) break;
            }
        }
        catch (ObjectDisposedException) { }
        catch (IOException) { }

        return sb.ToString();
    }

    private string ExtractValueFromResponse(string rawResponse)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(rawResponse)) return string.Empty;
        int equalSignIndex = rawResponse.IndexOf('=');
        if (equalSignIndex == -1) return rawResponse.Trim();

        string valuePart = rawResponse.Substring(equalSignIndex + 1);
        string[] lines = valuePart.Split(new[] { "\r", "\n" }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
        return lines.Length > 0 ? lines[0].Trim() : valuePart.Trim();
    }

    private string ProcessValue(string value, ParamConfig param)
    {
        if (param.Uncorrect != null && value.Equals(param.Uncorrect, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return "n/a";

        if (param.Annotations != null && param.Annotations.Count > 0)
        {
            if (param.Annotations.TryGetValue(value, out var interpretedValue))
                return interpretedValue;
            return "n/a";
        }

        return value;
    }
}

<file path="Signal.Server/Signal.Server/Controllers/AuthController.cs">
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Authorization;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Signal.Server.Data.Dtos;
using Signal.Server.Services;
using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
using System.Security.Claims;

namespace SignalTrack.Api.Controllers;

[ApiController]
[Route("api/auth")]
public class AuthController(AuthService authService, AuditLogService auditLogService) : ControllerBase
{
    private readonly AuthService authService = authService;
    private readonly AuditLogService auditLogService = auditLogService;

    private int? GetCurrentUserId()
    {
        var sub = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)
                  ?? User.FindFirstValue(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Sub)
                  ?? User.FindFirstValue("sub");
        return int.TryParse(sub, out int id) ? id : null;
    }

    private string? GetClientIp() => HttpContext.Connection.RemoteIpAddress?.ToString();

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]
    [HttpGet("{id}")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UserDto>> GetUserAsync(int id)
    {
        var user = await authService.GetUserAsync(id);
        if (user is null)
            return BadRequest("Пользователь не найден");
        return Ok(user);
    }

    [Authorize]
    [HttpGet]
    public async Task<ActionResult<UsersResponce>> GetUsersAsync([FromQuery] int page = 1, [FromQuery] int pageSize = 10)
    {
        return await authService.GetUsersAsync(page, pageSize);
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]
    [HttpGet("roles")]
    public async Task<ActionResult<List<string>>> GetRolesAsync()
    {
        return await authService.GetRolesAsync();
    }

    [HttpPost("login")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Login([FromBody] LoginRequest payload, [FromHeader(Name = "X-Device-Name")] string? deviceName)
    {
        var device = string.IsNullOrEmpty(deviceName) ? "Unknown Android Device" : Uri.UnescapeDataString(deviceName);

        var tokens = await authService.AuthenticateUserAsync(payload.Login, payload.Password, device);
        if (tokens == null) return Unauthorized(new { detail = "Неверный логин или пароль" });
        return Ok(tokens);
    }

    [HttpPost("logout")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Logout([FromBody] RefreshRequest payload)
    {
        await authService.InvalidateSessionAsync(payload.RefreshToken);
        return Ok(new { message = "Сессия успешно удалена" });
    }

    [HttpPost("refresh")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Refresh([FromBody] RefreshRequest payload)
    {
        var newTokens = await authService.RefreshUserTokensAsync(payload.RefreshToken);
        if (newTokens is null)
        {
            return Unauthorized(new { detail = "Невалидный refresh-токен. Требуется повторный вход." });
        }
        return Ok(newTokens);
    }

    [HttpPost("register")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> Register([FromBody] RegisterRequest dto)
    {
        var createdUserId = await authService.RegisterUserAsync(dto);
        if (createdUserId is null)
        {
            return BadRequest(new { detail = "Ошибка создания пользователя" });
        }

        var assignedRole = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dto.Role) ? "Инженер" : dto.Role.Trim();

        await auditLogService.LogActionAsync(
            GetCurrentUserId(),
            "CREATE_USER",
            "user",
            createdUserId.Value,
            new
            {
                login = dto.Login,
                firstName = dto.FirstName,
                secondName = dto.SecondName,
                lastName = dto.LastName,
                roles = new[] { assignedRole }
            },
            GetClientIp());

        return StatusCode(201, new { userId = createdUserId.Value, message = "Пользователь успешно зарегистрирован" });
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]
    [HttpPost("{id}/reset-password")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> ResetPassword(int id, [FromBody] string newPassworrd)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(newPassworrd))
        {
            return BadRequest(new { message = "Новый пароль не может быть пустым" });
        }

        var result = await authService.ResetPasswordAsync(id, newPassworrd);
        if (!result)
        {
            return NotFound(new { message = "Пользователь не найден" });
        }

        await auditLogService.LogActionAsync(
            GetCurrentUserId(),
            "RESET_PASSWORD",
            "user",
            id,
            new { targetUserId = id },
            GetClientIp());

        return Ok(new { message = "Пароль пользователя успешно изменен" });
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]
    [HttpPut("{id}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateUser(int id, [FromBody] UserDto dto)
    {
        try
        {
            var result = await authService.UpdateUserAsync(id, dto);
            if (!result) return NotFound(new { message = "Пользователь не найден" });

            await auditLogService.LogActionAsync(
                GetCurrentUserId(),
                "UPDATE_USER",
                "user",
                id,
                new { login = dto.Login, firstName = dto.FirstName, secondName = dto.SecondName, lastName = dto.LastName },
                GetClientIp());

            return Ok(new { message = "Данные успешно обновлены" });
        }
        catch (InvalidOperationException ex)
        {
            return BadRequest(new { message = ex.Message });
        }
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]
    [HttpDelete("{id}")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> DeleteUser(int id)
    {
        var result = await authService.DeleteUserAsync(id);
        if (!result) return NotFound(new { message = "Пользователь не найден" });

        await auditLogService.LogActionAsync(
            GetCurrentUserId(),
            "DELETE_USER",
            "user",
            id,
            new { deletedUserId = id },
            GetClientIp());

        return NoContent();
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]
    [HttpPut("{id}/roles")]
    public async Task<IActionResult> UpdateRoles(int id, [FromBody] UpdateUserRolesDto dto)
    {
        if (dto.Roles is null)
        {
            return BadRequest(new { message = "Список ролей не может быть пустым" });
        }

        var result = await authService.UpdateUserRolesAsync(id, dto.Roles);
        if (!result)
        {
            return NotFound(new { message = "Пользователь не найден" });
        }

        await auditLogService.LogActionAsync(
            GetCurrentUserId(),
            "UPDATE_USER_ROLES",
            "user_role",
            id,
            new { targetUserId = id, roles = dto.Roles },
            GetClientIp());

        return Ok(new { message = "Роли успешно обновлены" });
    }
}

{

    private readonly AuditLogService auditLogService = auditLogService;

    private int? GetCurrentUserId()
    {
        var sub = User.FindFirstValue(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)
                  ?? User.FindFirstValue(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Sub)
                  ?? User.FindFirstValue("sub");
        return int.TryParse(sub, out int id) ? id : null;
    }

        return Ok(points);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> GetEquipment()

        return Ok(devices);

        return Ok(devices);

        var readingTypes = await equipmentService.GetReadingTypesAsync(page, pageSize);
        return Ok(readingTypes);
    }

    public async Task<IActionResult> Create([FromBody] CreateReadingLogDto dto)
    {
        try
        {
            var result = await equipmentService.AddReadingLogAsync(dto);
            return StatusCode(201, new { result.ReadingLogId, result.StatusId, result.WriteDateTime });
        }
        catch (KeyNotFoundException ex)
        {
            return NotFound(new { message = ex.Message });
        }
        catch

            return BadRequest();
        }
    }

    {

        {
            int processed = await equipmentService.AddReadingLogsBatchAsync(dtos);
            return StatusCode(201, new { processed });

        catch (Exception ex)

            return BadRequest(new { message = ex.Message });

            GetCurrentUserId(),
            "IMPORT_AGENT_CONFIG",
            "equipment_command",
            null,
            new { devicesCount = devices?.Count ?? 0, updatedDevices = updated },
            GetClientIp());

        return Ok(new { updated });

    {

        if (!exists)

            return NotFound($"Прибор с серийным номером '{serialNumber}' не найден.");
        }

        var readings = await equipmentService.GetLatestReadingsAsync(serialNumber);
        return Ok(readings);

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]
    [HttpPost]
    public async Task<IActionResult> CreateEquipment([FromBody] EquipmentDto dto)
    {
        try

            var createdId = await equipmentService.CreateEquipmentAsync(dto);
            if (createdId is null)
                return BadRequest(new { message = "Ошибка добавления оборудования" });

            await auditLogService.LogActionAsync(
                GetCurrentUserId(),
                "CREATE_EQUIPMENT",
                "equipment",
                createdId.Value,
                dto with { EquipmentId = createdId.Value },
                GetClientIp());

            return StatusCode(201, new { equipmentId = createdId.Value, message = "Оборудование успешно добавлено" });
        }
        catch (InvalidOperationException ex)
        {
            return BadRequest(new { message = ex.Message });
        }
    }

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]

    {

        if (!result) return NotFound(new { message = "Оборудование не найдено" });

        await auditLogService.LogActionAsync(

    [Authorize(Roles = "Администратор")]

    {

using System.Text.Json.Serialization;

    );

using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace Signal.Server.Data.Dtos
{
    public class CreateReadingLogDto
    {
        public string IpAddress { get; set; } = null!;
        public string ReadingTypeIdentifier { get; set; } = null!;
        public string Value { get; set; } = null!;
        public DateTime? WriteDateTime { get; set; }
    }

    public class AgentParamConfigDto
    {
        [JsonPropertyName("Command")]
        public string Command { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        [JsonPropertyName("Name")]
        public string Name { get; set; } = null!;

        [JsonPropertyName("Uncorrect")]
        public string? Uncorrect { get; set; }

        [JsonPropertyName("PollIntervalSeconds")]
        public int PollIntervalSeconds { get; set; } = 30;

        [JsonPropertyName("Annotations")]
        public Dictionary<string, string>? Annotations { get; set; }
    }

    public class AgentDeviceConfigDto
    {
        [JsonPropertyName("Ip")]
        public string Ip { get; set; } = string.Empty;

        [JsonPropertyName("Login")]

        public string? Password { get; set; }

    );

        [property: JsonPropertyName("alertId")] int AlertId,
        [property: JsonPropertyName("equipmentId")] int EquipmentId,
        [property: JsonPropertyName("serialNumber")] string SerialNumber,

using System;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace Signal.Server.Data.Models;

[Table("admin_audit_log")]
[Index("CreatedAt", Name = "idx_audit_created_at", AllDescending = true)]
[Index("UserId", Name = "idx_audit_user_id")]
public partial class AdminAuditLog
{
    [Key]
    [Column("audit_log_id")]
    public int AuditLogId { get; set; }

    [Column("user_id")]
    public int? UserId { get; set; }

    [Column("action")]
    [StringLength(100)]
    public string Action { get; set; } = null!;

    [Column("entity_name")]
    [StringLength(50)]
    public string EntityName { get; set; } = null!;

    [Column("entity_id")]
    public int? EntityId { get; set; }

    [Column("details", TypeName = "jsonb")]
    public string? Details { get; set; }

    [Column("ip_address")]
    [StringLength(45)]
    public string? IpAddress { get; set; }

    [Column("created_at")]
    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; }

    [ForeignKey("UserId")]
    [InverseProperty("AdminAuditLogs")]
    public virtual User? User { get; set; }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.Server/Signal.Server/Data/Models/Alert.cs">
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace Signal.Server.Data.Models;

[Table("alert")]
public partial class Alert
{
    [Key]
    [Column("alert_id")]
    public int AlertId { get; set; }

    [Column("equipment_id")]
    public int EquipmentId { get; set; }

    [Column("reading_log_id")]
    public int? ReadingLogId { get; set; }

    [Column("severity")]
    [StringLength(20)]
    public string Severity { get; set; } = "WARNING";

    [Column("message")]
    [StringLength(255)]
    public string Message { get; set; } = null!;

    [Column("is_resolved")]
    public bool IsResolved { get; set; }

    [Column("created_at")]
    public DateTime CreatedAt { get; set; }

    [Column("resolved_at")]
    public DateTime? ResolvedAt { get; set; }

    [ForeignKey("EquipmentId")]
    [InverseProperty("Alerts")]
    public virtual Equipment Equipment { get; set; } = null!;

    [ForeignKey("ReadingLogId")]
    [InverseProperty("Alerts")]
    public virtual ReadingLog? ReadingLog { get; set; }

    [InverseProperty("Alert")]
    public virtual ICollection<UserAlert> UserAlerts { get; set; } = new List<UserAlert>();
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.Server/Signal.Server/Data/ApplicationDbContext.cs">
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Signal.Server.Data.Models;

namespace Signal.Server.Data;

public partial class ApplicationDbContext : DbContext
{
    public ApplicationDbContext()
    {
    }

    public ApplicationDbContext(DbContextOptions<ApplicationDbContext> options)
        : base(options)
    {
    }

    public virtual DbSet<AdminAuditLog> AdminAuditLogs { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Alert> Alerts { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Equipment> Equipment { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<EquipmentCommand> EquipmentCommands { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<ReadingLog> ReadingLogs { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<ReadingType> ReadingTypes { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Role> Roles { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Session> Sessions { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Status> Statuses { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<User> Users { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<UserAlert> UserAlerts { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<ViewLatestEquipmentReading> ViewLatestEquipmentReadings { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<ViewNewEquipment> ViewNewEquipments { get; set; }

    protected override void OnConfiguring(DbContextOptionsBuilder optionsBuilder)
    {
        if (!optionsBuilder.IsConfigured)
        {
            optionsBuilder.UseNpgsql("Host=localhost;Port=5432;Database=signal_track_db;Username=user1;Password=123");
        }
    }

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.Entity<AdminAuditLog>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.AuditLogId).HasName("admin_audit_log_pkey");

            entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt).HasDefaultValueSql("now()");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.User).WithMany(p => p.AdminAuditLogs)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("fk_audit_user");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Alert>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.AlertId).HasName("alert_pkey");

            entity.HasIndex(e => e.EquipmentId, "idx_alert_equipment_active")
                .HasFilter("(is_resolved = false)");

            entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt).HasDefaultValueSql("now()");
            entity.Property(e => e.IsResolved).HasDefaultValue(false);
            entity.Property(e => e.Severity).HasDefaultValueSql("'WARNING'::character varying");

            entity.ToTable("alert", t => t.HasCheckConstraint(
                "chk_alert_severity",
                "severity::text = ANY (ARRAY['INFO'::character varying, 'WARNING'::character varying, 'CRITICAL'::character varying, 'FATAL'::character varying]::text[])"));

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Equipment).WithMany(p => p.Alerts)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade)
                .HasConstraintName("fk_alert_equipment");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.ReadingLog).WithMany(p => p.Alerts)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.SetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("fk_alert_reading_log");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Equipment>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.EquipmentId).HasName("equipment_pkey");

            entity.Property(e => e.IsDeleted).HasDefaultValue(false);
            entity.Property(e => e.PollEnabled).HasDefaultValue(true);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Status).WithMany(p => p.Equipment)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict)
                .HasConstraintName("equipment_status_id_fkey");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<EquipmentCommand>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.CommandId).HasName("equipment_command_pkey");

            entity.Property(e => e.PollIntervalSeconds).HasDefaultValue(30);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Equipment).WithMany(p => p.EquipmentCommands)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade)
                .HasConstraintName("fk_cmd_equipment");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.ReadingType).WithMany(p => p.EquipmentCommands)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict)
                .HasConstraintName("fk_cmd_reading_type");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ReadingLog>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.ReadingLogId).HasName("reading_log_pkey");

            entity.Property(e => e.WriteDateTime).HasDefaultValueSql("now()");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Equipment).WithMany(p => p.ReadingLogs)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade)
                .HasConstraintName("reading_log_equipment_id_fkey");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.ReadingType).WithMany(p => p.ReadingLogs)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict)
                .HasConstraintName("reading_log_reading_type_id_fkey");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Status).WithMany(p => p.ReadingLogs)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Restrict)
                .HasConstraintName("reading_log_status_id_fkey");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ReadingType>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.ReadingTypeId).HasName("reading_type_pkey");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Role>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.RoleId).HasName("role_pkey");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Session>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.SessionId).HasName("session_pkey");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.User).WithMany(p => p.Sessions)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade)
                .HasConstraintName("session_user_id_fkey");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Status>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.StatusId).HasName("status_pkey");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<User>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.UserId).HasName("user_pkey");

            entity.HasMany(d => d.Roles).WithMany(p => p.Users)
                .UsingEntity<Dictionary<string, object>>(
                    "UserRole",
                    r => r.HasOne<Role>().WithMany()
                        .HasForeignKey("RoleId")
                        .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade)
                        .HasConstraintName("user_role_role_id_fkey"),
                    l => l.HasOne<User>().WithMany()
                        .HasForeignKey("UserId")
                        .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade)
                        .HasConstraintName("user_role_user_id_fkey"),
                    j =>
                    {
                        j.HasKey("UserId", "RoleId").HasName("user_role_pkey");
                        j.ToTable("user_role");
                        j.IndexerProperty<int>("UserId").HasColumnName("user_id");
                        j.IndexerProperty<int>("RoleId").HasColumnName("role_id");
                    });
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<UserAlert>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.UserAlertId).HasName("user_alert_pkey");

            entity.HasIndex(e => e.UserId, "idx_user_alert_unread")
                .HasFilter("(is_read = false)");

            entity.Property(e => e.IsRead).HasDefaultValue(false);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Alert).WithMany(p => p.UserAlerts)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade)
                .HasConstraintName("fk_user_alert_alert");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.User).WithMany(p => p.UserAlerts)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.Cascade)
                .HasConstraintName("fk_user_alert_user");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ViewLatestEquipmentReading>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToView("view_latest_equipment_readings");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ViewNewEquipment>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToView("view_new_equipment");
        });

        OnModelCreatingPartial(modelBuilder);
    }

    partial void OnModelCreatingPartial(ModelBuilder modelBuilder);
}

<file path="Signal.Server/Signal.Server/Services/AuditLogService.cs">
using System.Text.Encodings.Web;
using System.Text.Json;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Signal.Server.Data;
using Signal.Server.Data.Dtos;
using Signal.Server.Data.Models;

namespace Signal.Server.Services
{
    public class AuditLogService(ApplicationDbContext context)
    {
        private readonly ApplicationDbContext context = context;

        private static readonly JsonSerializerOptions JsonOptions = new()
        {
            Encoder = JavaScriptEncoder.UnsafeRelaxedJsonEscaping
        };

        public async Task LogActionAsync(
            int? userId,
            string action,
            string entityName,
            int? entityId,
            object? detailsObj,
            string? ipAddress)
        {
            try
            {
                string? detailsJson = detailsObj != null
                    ? JsonSerializer.Serialize(detailsObj, JsonOptions)
                    : null;

                var entry = new AdminAuditLog
                {
                    UserId = userId,
                    Action = action,
                    EntityName = entityName,
                    EntityId = entityId,
                    Details = detailsJson,
                    IpAddress = ipAddress,
                    CreatedAt = DateTime.UtcNow
                };

                context.AdminAuditLogs.Add(entry);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Ошибка записи в журнал аудита: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        public async Task<PagedAuditLogsResponse> GetAuditLogsAsync(
            int page = 1,
            int pageSize = 15,
            string? entityName = null,
            string? actionFilter = null,
            string? userLogin = null)
        {
            var query = context.AdminAuditLogs
                .Include(a => a.User)
                .AsQueryable();

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(entityName))
            {
                query = query.Where(a => a.EntityName.ToLower().Contains(entityName.ToLower()));
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(actionFilter))
            {
                query = query.Where(a => a.Action.ToLower().Contains(actionFilter.ToLower()));
            }

            if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(userLogin))
            {
                query = query.Where(a => a.User != null && a.User.Login.ToLower().Contains(userLogin.ToLower()));
            }

            int totalCount = await query.CountAsync();

            var logs = await query
                .OrderByDescending(a => a.CreatedAt)
                .Skip((page - 1) * pageSize)
                .Take(pageSize)
                .Select(a => new AdminAuditLogDto(
                    a.AuditLogId,
                    a.UserId,
                    a.User != null ? a.User.Login : "system",
                    a.Action,
                    a.EntityName,
                    a.EntityId,
                    a.Details,
                    a.IpAddress,
                    a.CreatedAt
                ))
                .ToListAsync();

            return new PagedAuditLogsResponse
            {
                Logs = logs,
                TotalCount = totalCount
            };
        }
    }
}
</file>

<file path="Signal.Server/Signal.Server/Services/AuthService.cs">
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.IdentityModel.Tokens;
using Signal.Server.Data;
using Signal.Server.Data.Dtos;
using Signal.Server.Data.Models;
using System.Data;
using System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt;
using System.Security.Claims;

namespace Signal.Server.Services
{
    public class AuthService(ApplicationDbContext context)
    {
        private readonly ApplicationDbContext context = context;

        private byte[] key = AuthOptions.keyBytes;

        public bool VerifyPassword(string inputPassword, string hashedPassword)
            => BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.Verify(inputPassword, hashedPassword);

        public string HashRawPassword(string password)
            => BCrypt.Net.BCrypt.HashPassword(password, AuthOptions.workFactor);

        public TokenResponse GenerateJwtTokens(int userId,
                                               string? first,
                                               string? last,
                                               string? middle,
                                               List<Role> roles)
        {
            var tokenHandler = new JwtSecurityTokenHandler();

            var claims = new List<Claim>
            {
                new Claim(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Sub, userId.ToString())
            };

            var roleNames = new List<string>();
            foreach (var role in roles)
            {
                claims.Add(new Claim(ClaimTypes.Role, role.Name));
                roleNames.Add(role.Name);
            }

            var tokenDescriptor = new SecurityTokenDescriptor
            {
                Subject = new ClaimsIdentity(claims, "Bearer"),
                Expires = DateTime.UtcNow.AddMinutes(15),
                Issuer = AuthOptions.validIssuer,
                Audience = AuthOptions.validAudience,
                SigningCredentials = new SigningCredentials(new SymmetricSecurityKey(key), AuthOptions.algorithm)
            };
            var accessToken = tokenHandler.CreateToken(tokenDescriptor);

            var refreshDescriptor = new SecurityTokenDescriptor
            {
                Subject = new ClaimsIdentity(new[] { new Claim(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Sub, userId.ToString()) }),
                Expires = DateTime.UtcNow.AddDays(3),
                Issuer = AuthOptions.validIssuer,
                Audience = AuthOptions.validAudience,
                SigningCredentials = new SigningCredentials(new SymmetricSecurityKey(key), AuthOptions.algorithm)
            };
            var refreshToken = tokenHandler.CreateToken(refreshDescriptor);

            return new TokenResponse(
                tokenHandler.WriteToken(accessToken),
                tokenHandler.WriteToken(refreshToken),
                first, last, middle, roleNames
            );
        }

        public async Task<UserDto?> GetUserAsync(int id)
        {
            return await context.Users
                .Where(u => u.UserId == id)
                .Select(u => new UserDto
                {
                    UserId = u.UserId,
                    Login = u.Login,
                    FirstName = u.FirstName,
                    LastName = u.LastName,
                    SecondName = u.SecondName
                }).FirstOrDefaultAsync();
        }

        public async Task<UsersResponce> GetUsersAsync(int page, int pageSize)
        {
            var usersCount = context.Users.Count();
            var users = await context.Users
                .Include(u => u.Roles)
                .Select(u => new UserDto
                {
                    UserId = u.UserId,
                    Login = u.Login,
                    FirstName = u.FirstName,
                    LastName = u.LastName,
                    SecondName = u.SecondName,
                    RoleNames = u.Roles.Select(r => r.Name).ToList()
                }).Skip(pageSize * (page - 1)).Take(pageSize).ToListAsync();

            return new UsersResponce()
            {
                Users = users,
                TotalUsers = usersCount
            };
        }

        public async Task<TokenResponse?> AuthenticateUserAsync(string login, string passwordRaw, string deviceName)
        {
            var user = await context.Users.Include(r => r.Roles).FirstOrDefaultAsync(u => u.Login == login);
            if (user is null || !VerifyPassword(passwordRaw, user.HashedPassword))
            {
                return null;
            }

            var tokens = GenerateJwtTokens(user.UserId, user.FirstName, user.SecondName, user.LastName, user.Roles.ToList());

            var session = new Session
            {
                UserId = user.UserId,
                RefreshToken = tokens.RefreshToken,
                Device = deviceName,
                SessionEndDateTime = DateTime.UtcNow.AddDays(3)
            };

            try
            {
                context.Sessions.Add(session);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();
            }
            catch
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Ошибка добавления сессии для {session.UserId}");
            }

            return tokens;
        }

        public async Task<TokenResponse?> RefreshUserTokensAsync(string refreshToken)
        {
            try
            {
                var tokenHandler = new JwtSecurityTokenHandler();
                var validationParameters = new TokenValidationParameters
                {
                    ValidateIssuerSigningKey = true,
                    IssuerSigningKey = new SymmetricSecurityKey(key),
                    ValidIssuer = AuthOptions.validIssuer,
                    ValidAudience = AuthOptions.validAudience,
                    ClockSkew = TimeSpan.Zero
                };

                var principal = tokenHandler.ValidateToken(refreshToken, validationParameters, out _);
                var userIdStr = principal.FindFirst(ClaimTypes.NameIdentifier)?.Value
                ?? principal.FindFirst("sub")?.Value
                ?? principal.FindFirst(JwtRegisteredClaimNames.Sub)?.Value;

                if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(userIdStr) || !int.TryParse(userIdStr, out int userId)) return null;

                var now = DateTime.UtcNow;
                var session = await context.Sessions
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.UserId == userId
                    && s.RefreshToken == refreshToken
                    && s.SessionEndDateTime > now);

                if (session is null) return null;

                var user = await context.Users
                    .Include(u => u.Roles)
                    .FirstOrDefaultAsync(u => u.UserId == userId);

                if (user is null) return null;

                var newTokens = GenerateJwtTokens(userId, user.FirstName, user.SecondName, user.LastName, user.Roles.ToList());

                session.RefreshToken = newTokens.RefreshToken;
                session.SessionEndDateTime = DateTime.UtcNow.AddDays(3);

                await context.SaveChangesAsync();
                return newTokens;
            }
            catch { return null; }
        }

        public async Task<bool> InvalidateSessionAsync(string refreshToken)
        {
            var session = await context.Sessions.FirstOrDefaultAsync(s => s.RefreshToken == refreshToken);
            if (session is not null)
            {
                context.Sessions.Remove(session);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();
                return true;
            }
            return false;
        }

        public async Task<int?> RegisterUserAsync(RegisterRequest dto)
        {
            var hashedPassword = HashRawPassword(dto.HashedPassword);
            var targetRoleName = string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(dto.Role) ? "Инженер" : dto.Role.Trim();

            try
            {
                var assignedRoles = await context.Roles
                    .Where(r => r.Name == targetRoleName)
                    .ToListAsync();

                if (assignedRoles.Count == 0)
                {
                    assignedRoles = await context.Roles
                        .Where(r => r.Name == "Инженер")
                        .ToListAsync();
                }

                var newUser = new User
                {
                    Login = dto.Login,
                    HashedPassword = hashedPassword,
                    FirstName = dto.FirstName,
                    SecondName = dto.SecondName,
                    LastName = dto.LastName,
                    Roles = assignedRoles
                };

                context.Users.Add(newUser);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();
                return newUser.UserId;
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Ошибка регистрации пользователя: {ex.Message}");
                return null;
            }
        }

        public async Task<bool> UpdateUserAsync(int id, UserDto dto)
        {
            var user = await context.Users.FindAsync(id);
            if (user is null) return false;

            if (user.Login != dto.Login)
            {
                bool loginExists = await context.Users.AnyAsync(u => u.Login == dto.Login);
                if (loginExists) throw new InvalidOperationException("Логин уже занят");
            }

            user.Login = dto.Login;
            user.SecondName = dto.SecondName;
            user.FirstName = dto.FirstName;
            user.LastName = dto.LastName;

            try
            {
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();
                return true;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public async Task<bool> ResetPasswordAsync(int id, string newPassword)
        {
            var hashedNewPassword = HashRawPassword(newPassword);

            var user = new User { UserId = id };

            context.Entry(user).Property(u => u.HashedPassword).CurrentValue = hashedNewPassword;
            context.Entry(user).Property(u => u.HashedPassword).IsModified = true;

            try
            {
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();
                return true;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public async Task<bool> DeleteUserAsync(int id)
        {
            var user = await context.Users.FindAsync(id);
            if (user is null) return false;

            try
            {
                context.Users.Remove(user);
                await context.SaveChangesAsync();
                return true;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }

        public async Task<bool> UpdateUserRolesAsync(int userId, List<string> newRoles)
        {
            var user = await context.Users
                .Include(u => u.Roles)
                .FirstOrDefaultAsync(u => u.UserId == userId);

            if (user is null) return false;

            user.Roles.Clear();

            var roles = await context.Roles.ToListAsync();

            var roleIds = new List<int>();
            foreach (var role in newRoles)
            {
                var matchedRole = roles.FirstOrDefault(r => r.Name == role);
                if (matchedRole != null && matchedRole.RoleId != 0)
                    roleIds.Add(matchedRole.RoleId);
            }

            if (newRoles is not null && newRoles.Count > 0)
            {
                var rolesToAdd = await context.Roles
                    .Where(r => roleIds.Contains(r.RoleId))
                    .ToListAsync();

                foreach (var role in rolesToAdd)
                {
                    user.Roles.Add(role);
                }
            }

            context.Entry(user).State = EntityState.Modified;
            await context.SaveChangesAsync();

            return true;
        }

        public async Task<ActionResult<List<string>>> GetRolesAsync()
        {
            var roles = await context.Roles.ToListAsync();
            if (roles is null) return new List<string>();

            var roleNames = new List<string>();
            foreach (var role in roles)
                roleNames.Add(role.Name);

            return roleNames;
        }
    }
}
